## Psalms 11:1–7 (id: 23825)

The psalmist affirms his faith in the righteous God. Even though the world appears hopelessly chaotic, both the godly and the wicked can depend on God’s justice. The godly can depend on him as a refuge and shelter.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 11:1-7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`); David (ID: `person:David`); Arrow (ID: `keyterm:Arrow`); Throne (ID: `realia:Throne`); Bird (ID: `fauna:Bird`); Arrow (ID: `realia:Arrow`); Bow (ID: `realia:Bow`); Trap (ID: `realia:Trap`)

## Psalms 11:2 (id: 23828)

The wicked live in the shadows and create gloom for the godly ([Psalms 82:5](https://ref.ly/Ps82:5); [139:11](https://ref.ly/Ps139:11); [143:3](https://ref.ly/Ps143:3); [Proverbs 4:19](https://ref.ly/Prov4:19)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 11:2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Arrow (ID: `keyterm:Arrow`); Arrow (ID: `realia:Arrow`); Bow (ID: `realia:Bow`)

## Psalms 11:2–3 (id: 23827)

In the ongoing struggle between those who do evil and those who follow God, the wicked often harm the righteous. This can leave the godly confused and full of questions.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 11:2-3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Arrow (ID: `keyterm:Arrow`); Arrow (ID: `realia:Arrow`); Bow (ID: `realia:Bow`)

## Psalms 11:3 (id: 23829)

The wicked create a world of darkness (see [Psalms 82:5](https://ref.ly/Ps82:5)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 11:3


## Psalms 11:4 (id: 23830)

The righteous King rules from heaven, where his throne remains free from human corruption (see [Psalm 2:4](https://ref.ly/Ps2:4)). He examines everyone and sees everything that happens on earth (see [Psalm 14:2–3](https://ref.ly/Ps14:2-Ps14:3)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 11:4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Throne (ID: `realia:Throne`)

## Psalms 11:5 (id: 23831)

* *hates*: See [Psalm 5:4–6](https://ref.ly/Ps5:4-Ps5:6); [Proverbs 6:16–18](https://ref.ly/Prov6:16-Prov6:18).
* Wicked deeds result in destruction and violence ([Psalms 58:2](https://ref.ly/Ps58:2); [73:6](https://ref.ly/Ps73:6); [74:20](https://ref.ly/Ps74:20)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 11:5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 11:6 (id: 23832)

God sent fiery coals and burning sulfur on Sodom and Gomorrah ([Genesis 19](https://ref.ly/Gen19:1-Gen19:38)) as symbols of judgment ([Psalms 18:8](https://ref.ly/Ps18:8); [120:4](https://ref.ly/Ps120:4); [140:10](https://ref.ly/Ps140:10)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 11:6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Trap (ID: `realia:Trap`)

## Psalms 11:7 (id: 23833)

The godly are assured of the Lord’s favorable presence when they see his face (see [Psalm 23:6](https://ref.ly/Ps23:6)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 11:7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 12:1–4 (id: 23962)

Evil becomes so widespread that the godly pray for rescue ([Isaiah 57:1](https://ref.ly/Isa57:1); [Micah 7:2](https://ref.ly/Mic7:2); compare [Psalms 1:6](https://ref.ly/Ps1:6); [37:20](https://ref.ly/Ps37:20); [58:7](https://ref.ly/Ps58:7); [104:35](https://ref.ly/Ps104:35)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 12:1-4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Loyal Person (ID: `keyterm:LoyalPerson`); Faithfulness (ID: `keyterm:Faithfulness`)

## Psalms 12:1–8 (id: 23963)

This prayer from the community asks the Lord for help against the many evil acts in the world. Because God answers, the godly can face the future with confidence, knowing that God protects them.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 12:1-8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Pure (ID: `keyterm:Pure`); Faithfulness (ID: `keyterm:Faithfulness`); Loyal Person (ID: `keyterm:LoyalPerson`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Kiln (ID: `realia:Kiln`); Money (ID: `realia:Money`)

## Psalms 12:4 (id: 23964)

The words they say reveal the character of the wicked. They deceive and overpower the godly and arrogantly question the Lord’s purposes.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 12:4


## Psalms 12:6 (id: 23965)

* The godly can trust the Lord's promises, but not the lies of the wicked ([Psalm 12:2–4](https://ref.ly/Ps12:2-Ps12:4)).
* *Sevenfold* means entirely or completely.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 12:6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Pure (ID: `keyterm:Pure`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Kiln (ID: `realia:Kiln`); Money (ID: `realia:Money`)

## Psalms 12:7–8 (id: 23966)

Those who follow God know that he cares, even when the wicked oppress them. God sees the corrupt and foolish people who praise those who do evil (see [Psalms 9:19–20](https://ref.ly/Ps9:19-Ps9:20); [14:2–6](https://ref.ly/Ps14:2-Ps14:6)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 12:7-8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 13:1–2 (id: 24010)

*How long?*: Repeating the question four times shows intense worry and deep pain ([Psalm 79:5](https://ref.ly/Ps79:5)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 13:1-2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 13:1–6 (id: 24011)

The psalmist’s loud cry for help becomes a confident song of hope.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 13:1-6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); A Group of People (ID: `group:GenericGroup`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`)

## Psalms 13:2 (id: 24012)

A feeling of deep sadness and pain can make it hard to see any hope ([Psalm 6:3](https://ref.ly/Ps6:3)). Without God’s answer to his prayers ([Psalm 22:1](https://ref.ly/Ps22:1)), death seems unavoidable, painful, and slow ([Psalms 6:6](https://ref.ly/Ps6:6); [32:3](https://ref.ly/Ps32:3); [55:4](https://ref.ly/Ps55:4); [61:2](https://ref.ly/Ps61:2); [102:5](https://ref.ly/Ps102:5); [116:3](https://ref.ly/Ps116:3)). The psalmist struggles to pray and longs for God's redemption ([Psalms 25:17–18](https://ref.ly/Ps25:17-Ps25:18); [31:10](https://ref.ly/Ps31:10); [38:8](https://ref.ly/Ps38:8); [39:2](https://ref.ly/Ps39:2)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 13:2


## Psalms 13:3 (id: 24014)

The psalmist's suffering feels like death ([Psalms 22:15](https://ref.ly/Ps22:15); [55:4](https://ref.ly/Ps55:4); [88:15](https://ref.ly/Ps88:15); [94:17](https://ref.ly/Ps94:17); [116:3](https://ref.ly/Ps116:3)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 13:3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 13:3–4 (id: 24013)

Only the Lord can provide understanding and comfort. The psalmist hopes for the Lord's justice so his enemies will not celebrate.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 13:3-4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** A Group of People (ID: `group:GenericGroup`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 13:5–6 (id: 24015)

The psalmist no longer hears the mockery of his enemies. Instead, he feels joyful because he trusts in the Lord.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 13:5-6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 14:1 (id: 24085)

Also known as fools*,* the wicked talk to themselves, think, scheme ([Psalm 10:4](https://ref.ly/Ps10:4), [6](https://ref.ly/Ps10:6), [11](https://ref.ly/Ps10:11)), and make claims. However, because they ignore God’s revelation, they lack understanding ([Psalms 14:1](https://ref.ly/Ps14:1); [39:8](https://ref.ly/Ps39:8); [53:1](https://ref.ly/Ps53:1); [74:18](https://ref.ly/Ps74:18), [22](https://ref.ly/Ps74:22)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 14:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** A Fool (ID: `person:Fool`); David (ID: `person:David`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 14:1–3 (id: 24083)

Paul uses phrases from the Greek translation of this passage in his description of the wickedness of all people ([Romans 3:10–12](https://ref.ly/Rom3:10-Rom3:12)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 14:1-3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); A Fool (ID: `person:Fool`); David (ID: `person:David`); Have Insight (ID: `keyterm:HaveInsight`)

## Psalms 14:1–7 (id: 24084)

This wisdom psalm includes elements of lament and prophetic speech. The ideals of [Psalm 8](https://ref.ly/Ps8:1-Ps8:9) clash with foolish human attitudes and sinful actions. Except for verses [5–6](https://ref.ly/Ps14:5-Ps14:6), this psalm is similar to [Psalm 53](https://ref.ly/Ps53:1-Ps53:6).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 14:1-7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); A Fool (ID: `person:Fool`); Have Insight (ID: `keyterm:HaveInsight`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Counsel (ID: `keyterm:Counsel`); David (ID: `person:David`); Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); Refuge (ID: `keyterm:Refuge`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`)

## Psalms 14:2–3 (id: 24086)

From heaven ([Psalms 33:13](https://ref.ly/Ps33:13); [80:14](https://ref.ly/Ps80:14); [102:19](https://ref.ly/Ps102:19)), the Lord sees the wicked, who are clearly evil ([Psalm 11:3–5](https://ref.ly/Ps11:3-Ps11:5)). All humans lack wisdom and do not live in the way God originally intended for them ([Psalm 10:4–11](https://ref.ly/Ps10:4-Ps10:11); see [Romans 1–3](https://ref.ly/Rom1:1-Rom3:31)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 14:2-3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Have Insight (ID: `keyterm:HaveInsight`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 14:4 (id: 24087)

The evil live at the expense of others ([Psalms 27:2](https://ref.ly/Ps27:2); [44:11](https://ref.ly/Ps44:11); [53:4](https://ref.ly/Ps53:4); see [Isaiah 5:8](https://ref.ly/Isa5:8)) and have no reverence or knowledge of God ([Psalm 79:6](https://ref.ly/Ps79:6)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 14:4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 14:5 (id: 24088)

Though the wicked perish ([Psalm 1:5–6](https://ref.ly/Ps1:5-Ps1:6)), God is with the righteous.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 14:5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 14:7 (id: 24089)

* The psalmist prays for the renewal of God’s relationship with his people.
* *Jacob* is another name for *Israel* (see [Genesis 35:9–10](https://ref.ly/Gen35:9-Gen35:10)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 14:7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 15:1 (id: 24158)

* Only those who follow God may be in the Lord’s presence ([Psalm 5:4](https://ref.ly/Ps5:4)). As his guests, they experience his hospitality and protection.
* *tent*: In David’s time, God’s sanctuary was a tent (the tabernacle; see [1 Samuel 21:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:6); [2 Samuel 6:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam6:17); [7:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:6); [12:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:20); [1 Chronicles 6:32](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:32)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 15:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** David (ID: `person:David`); Psalm (ID: `keyterm:Psalm`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 15:1–5 (id: 24157)

This wisdom poem contrasts the lifestyle of the righteous with the actions of fools ([Psalm 14](https://ref.ly/Ps14:1-Ps14:7)). The questions in this psalm invite people to examine themselves and not pay attention to problems with the wicked. The question of who dwells in God’s presence leads into a description of godly character qualities (compare [Psalm 24:3–6](https://ref.ly/Ps24:3-Ps24:6); [Isaiah 33:14–16](https://ref.ly/Isa33:14-Isa33:16)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 15:1-5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Exempt (ID: `keyterm:Exempt`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); Psalm (ID: `keyterm:Psalm`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Complete (ID: `keyterm:Complete`); Bring Honor and Glory on Oneself (ID: `keyterm:BringHonorAndGloryOnOneself`); David (ID: `person:David`); Swear (ID: `keyterm:Swear`); Money (ID: `realia:Money`)

## Psalms 15:1–19.14 (id: 24156)

The psalms in this section emphasize integrity and purity ([Psalms 17:15](https://ref.ly/Ps17:15); [18:20–26](https://ref.ly/Ps18:20-Ps18:26); [19:13–14](https://ref.ly/Ps19:13-Ps19:14)). They describe people as living in God’s presence ([Psalms 16:9–11](https://ref.ly/Ps16:9-Ps16:11); [17:15](https://ref.ly/Ps17:15)) and remaining steadfast ([15:5](https://ref.ly/Ps15:5); [16:8](https://ref.ly/Ps16:8)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 15:1-19:14

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Complete (ID: `keyterm:Complete`); Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`); David (ID: `person:David`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Shield (ID: `keyterm:Shield`); Loyal Person (ID: `keyterm:LoyalPerson`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); The Way (ID: `keyterm:TheWay`); Hell (ID: `place:Hell`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Small Shield (ID: `realia:SmallShield`); Lot (ID: `keyterm:Lot`); Arrow (ID: `keyterm:Arrow`); Exempt (ID: `keyterm:Exempt`); Horn (ID: `keyterm:Horn`); Instruction (ID: `keyterm:Instruction`); Delight (ID: `keyterm:Delight`); Wickedness (ID: `keyterm:Wickedness`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Testimony (ID: `keyterm:Witness.2`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Be Blameless (ID: `keyterm:Blameless`); Bring Honor and Glory on Oneself (ID: `keyterm:BringHonorAndGloryOnOneself`); To Show Oneself Loyal (ID: `keyterm:ShowOneselfLoyal`); Intention (ID: `keyterm:Intention`); Safety (ID: `keyterm:Safety`); Simple Person (ID: `keyterm:SimplePerson`)

## Psalms 15:2 (id: 24159)

The godly speak truthfully and act faithfully in their relationships. Unlike the wicked, they are open and transparent ([Psalm 12:2–4](https://ref.ly/Ps12:2-Ps12:4)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 15:2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); Complete (ID: `keyterm:Complete`)

## Psalms 15:5 (id: 24160)

* The Lord prohibited Israel from charging interest to people in need ([Exodus 22:25–27](https://ref.ly/Exod22:25-Exod22:27)).
* The assurance of God’s presence keeps the godly from destruction ([Psalms 16:8](https://ref.ly/Ps16:8); [46:5](https://ref.ly/Ps46:5); [125:1](https://ref.ly/Ps125:1)). Their situations may temporarily unsettle them, but they ultimately remain strong ([Psalm 30:6](https://ref.ly/Ps30:6); [94:18](https://ref.ly/Ps94:18); [112:6](https://ref.ly/Ps112:6)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 15:5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Exempt (ID: `keyterm:Exempt`); Money (ID: `realia:Money`)

## Psalms 16:1 (id: 24168)

The psalmist feels safe because only the Lord offers: 

* security ([Psalm 16:5–6](https://ref.ly/Ps16:5-Ps16:6)),
* guidance ([Psalm 16:7–8](https://ref.ly/Ps16:7-Ps16:8)), and
* the joy of his presence ([Psalm 16:9–11](https://ref.ly/Ps16:9-Ps16:11)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 16:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** David (ID: `person:David`); Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 16:1–11 (id: 24165)

In this psalm of confidence, the poet quickly transitions from a brief request ([Psalm 16:1](https://ref.ly/Ps16:1)) to expressions of deep commitment to God and his people ([Psalm 16:2–6](https://ref.ly/Ps16:2-Ps16:6)). The psalm concludes with confident praise ([Psalm 16:7–11](https://ref.ly/Ps16:7-Ps16:11)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 16:1-11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Lot (ID: `keyterm:Lot`); Safety (ID: `keyterm:Safety`); Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); Mighty (ID: `keyterm:Mighty`); Hell (ID: `place:Hell`); Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`); Loyal Person (ID: `keyterm:LoyalPerson`); David (ID: `person:David`); Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); To Cause to Possess (ID: `keyterm:Possess`); Wine (ID: `realia:Wine`); Rope (ID: `realia:Rope`)

## Psalms 16:2 (id: 24169)

*I said to the LORD*: The various writers of the psalms pray, lament, and praise the Lord throughout the Psalter (the book of Psalms). The Lord answers them (see [Psalm 12:5–6](https://ref.ly/Ps12:5-Ps12:6)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 16:2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 16:4 (id: 24170)

The psalmist separates himself from unholy worship, rituals, and words (see [Psalm 15:3–5](https://ref.ly/Ps15:3-Ps15:5)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 16:4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); Wine (ID: `realia:Wine`)

## Psalms 16:5 (id: 24171)

The cup of blessing represents how God supplies all the psalmist's needs ([Psalm 23:5](https://ref.ly/Ps23:5); see [116:13](https://ref.ly/Ps116:13); compare [75:8](https://ref.ly/Ps75:8); [Isaiah 51:17](https://ref.ly/Isa51:17)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 16:5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Lot (ID: `keyterm:Lot`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 16:6 (id: 24172)

The land is God’s gift for his people ([Deuteronomy 18:8](https://ref.ly/Deut18:8)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 16:6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** To Cause to Possess (ID: `keyterm:Possess`); Rope (ID: `realia:Rope`)

## Psalms 16:7–8 (id: 24173)

* God's guidance gives life ([Psalm 16:11](https://ref.ly/Ps16:11); see [5:8](https://ref.ly/Ps5:8); [23:1–3](https://ref.ly/Ps23:1-Ps23:3); [32:8](https://ref.ly/Ps32:8); [73:24](https://ref.ly/Ps73:24)).
* The psalmist relies on the Lord for strength and joy, feeling God's presence beside him ([Psalm 16:11](https://ref.ly/Ps16:11); see [73:23](https://ref.ly/Ps73:23); [109:31](https://ref.ly/Ps109:31); [110:1](https://ref.ly/Ps110:1), [5](https://ref.ly/Ps110:5); [121:5](https://ref.ly/Ps121:5)).
* Peter quoted [Psalm 16:8–11](https://ref.ly/Ps16:8-Ps16:11) in his sermon on the day of Pentecost ([Acts 2:25–28](https://ref.ly/Acts2:25-Acts2:28)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 16:7-8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`)

## Psalms 16:9–11 (id: 24174)

The psalmist feels confident because he knows that even death cannot separate him from the Lord. He fears separation (see [Psalm 13:3–4](https://ref.ly/Ps13:3-Ps13:4)), but he remains confident that he will see the Lord (see [11:7](https://ref.ly/Ps11:7)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 16:9-11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Hell (ID: `place:Hell`); Safety (ID: `keyterm:Safety`); Loyal Person (ID: `keyterm:LoyalPerson`)

## Psalms 16:10 (id: 24166)

The psalmist shows belief that life goes on after death. The apostles applied this text to the resurrection of Jesus ([Acts 2:25–33](https://ref.ly/Acts2:25-Acts2:33); [13:35](https://ref.ly/Acts13:35)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 16:10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Hell (ID: `place:Hell`); Loyal Person (ID: `keyterm:LoyalPerson`)

## Psalms 16:11 (id: 24167)

*at Your right hand*: See [Psalm 16:7–8](https://ref.ly/Ps16:7-Ps16:8); [110:1](https://ref.ly/Ps110:1).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 16:11


## Psalms 17:1 (id: 24178)

*comes from lips free of deceit*: Deception characterizes the wicked. The righteous reject such deceit as a failure of character and pray for rescue from such people ([Psalms 5:6](https://ref.ly/Ps5:6); [43:1](https://ref.ly/Ps43:1); [120:2](https://ref.ly/Ps120:2)). They love honesty, truth, and integrity ([Psalms 15:2–3](https://ref.ly/Ps15:2-Ps15:3); [24:4](https://ref.ly/Ps24:4); [101:7](https://ref.ly/Ps101:7)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 17:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** David (ID: `person:David`); Deceit (ID: `keyterm:Deceit`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 17:1–15 (id: 24175)

The psalmist asks for God's protection and justice, affirms his own integrity, and prays for the Lord to defeat his strong enemies.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 17:1-15

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Fairness (ID: `keyterm:Fairness`); Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`); David (ID: `person:David`); Deceit (ID: `keyterm:Deceit`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Sword (ID: `realia:Sword`); Lion (ID: `fauna:Lion`)

## Psalms 17:3 (id: 24180)

Although he is not perfect, the psalmist dedicates himself to the Lord. He does not want to sin through lies, flattery, or gossip (see [Psalms 10:7](https://ref.ly/Ps10:7); [15:2–3](https://ref.ly/Ps15:2-Ps15:3); [17:1](https://ref.ly/Ps17:1)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 17:3


## Psalms 17:3–5 (id: 24179)

The psalmist declares his innocence ([Psalm 17:1](https://ref.ly/Ps17:1)) and commits himself to integrity by being open to God’s examination.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 17:3-5


## Psalms 17:6–9 (id: 24181)

This prayer for God's care continues the request begun in [Psalm 17:1–2](https://ref.ly/Ps17:1-Ps17:2) for redemption from evil.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 17:6-9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 17:8 (id: 24182)

* *as the apple of Your eye* (literally *as the pupil of your eye*): This metaphor shows God's care and love for the psalmist (see [Deuteronomy 32:10](https://ref.ly/Deut32:10)).
* The shadow of God's wings is a place of his protection from enemies and hardship ([Psalms 36:7](https://ref.ly/Ps36:7); [57:1](https://ref.ly/Ps57:1); [61:4](https://ref.ly/Ps61:4); [63:7](https://ref.ly/Ps63:7); [91:4](https://ref.ly/Ps91:4)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 17:8


## Psalms 17:13–14 (id: 24176)

The psalmist prays confidently for the Lord's victory, knowing God is more powerful than the evildoers. The psalmist finds joy in the Lord's inheritance ([Psalm 16:5](https://ref.ly/Ps16:5)), whereas the wicked search for joy in this world.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 17:13-14

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Sword (ID: `realia:Sword`)

## Psalms 17:15 (id: 24177)

*behold Your face*: The psalmist does not allow temporary concerns raised by adversaries to stand in the way of his experiencing a real and lasting relationship with the Lord.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 17:15


## Psalms 18:1 (id: 24186)

* The servant of the Lord was given the duty to establish God's kingdom on earth (see [Psalm 78:70](https://ref.ly/Ps78:70); [132:10](https://ref.ly/Ps132:10); [144:10](https://ref.ly/Ps144:10)).
* The Lord rescued David from all his enemies by helping him defeat them (see [2 Samuel 8:1–14](https://ref.ly/2Sam8:1-2Sam8:14)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 18:1–50 (id: 24183)

This psalm describes a royal story of God rescuing his king. A similar version of this psalm appears in [2 Samuel 22:1–51](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:1-2Sam22:51). The psalm encourages those who hear the earlier prayers for help in [Psalms 16](https://ref.ly/Ps16:1-Ps16:11) and [17](https://ref.ly/Ps17:1-Ps17:15). 

The psalmist describes the dramatic nature of God’s rescue in three different ways ([Psalm 18:7–15](https://ref.ly/Ps18:7-Ps18:15), [16–19](https://ref.ly/Ps18:16-Ps18:19), [30–36](https://ref.ly/Ps18:30-Ps18:36)). The psalm also offers hope that a future Son of David will completely defeat evil ([Psalm 18:43–50](https://ref.ly/Ps18:43-Ps18:50); compare [Psalm 2](https://ref.ly/Ps2:1-Ps2:12)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:1-50

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Complete (ID: `keyterm:Complete`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Shield (ID: `keyterm:Shield`); Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`); The Way (ID: `keyterm:TheWay`); Small Shield (ID: `realia:SmallShield`); Loyal Person (ID: `keyterm:LoyalPerson`); Horn (ID: `keyterm:Horn`); Arrow (ID: `keyterm:Arrow`); Wickedness (ID: `keyterm:Wickedness`); David (ID: `person:David`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); To Show Oneself Loyal (ID: `keyterm:ShowOneselfLoyal`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Anointed (ID: `keyterm:Anointed`); Force to Serve (ID: `keyterm:EnticedToServe`); To Act Wickedly (ID: `keyterm:ActWickedly`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Hell (ID: `place:Hell`); Foundation (ID: `realia:Foundation`); Trap (ID: `realia:Trap`); Fortress (ID: `realia:Fortress`); Bow (ID: `realia:Bow`); Winged Creature (ID: `realia:WingedCreature`); Deer (ID: `fauna:Deer`); Sheep (ID: `fauna:Lamb`); Lamp (ID: `realia:Lamp`)

## Psalms 18:2 (id: 24194)

A shield symbolizes protection, salvation, and victory ([Psalms 7:10](https://ref.ly/Ps7:10); [18:2](https://ref.ly/Ps18:2), [30](https://ref.ly/Ps18:30), [35](https://ref.ly/Ps18:35); [84:11](https://ref.ly/Ps84:11); [91:4](https://ref.ly/Ps91:4); [115:9](https://ref.ly/Ps115:9), [10](https://ref.ly/Ps115:10), [11](https://ref.ly/Ps115:11); [119:114](https://ref.ly/Ps119:114); [144:2](https://ref.ly/Ps144:2)). It inspires trust, patience, and confidence in God.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Horn (ID: `keyterm:Horn`); Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`); Fortress (ID: `realia:Fortress`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Shield (ID: `keyterm:Shield`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Sheep (ID: `fauna:Lamb`); Small Shield (ID: `realia:SmallShield`)

## Psalms 18:3 (id: 24200)

God deserves praise for his greatness and faithfulness ([Psalms 48:1](https://ref.ly/Ps48:1); [96:4](https://ref.ly/Ps96:4); [145:3](https://ref.ly/Ps145:3)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 18:6 (id: 24207)

God lives in his sanctuary, the heavenly temple ([Psalm 102:19](https://ref.ly/Ps102:19); see [14:2–3](https://ref.ly/Ps14:2-Ps14:3)). He knows everything and sees every person. He will defend and vindicate those who live in a faithful and righteous way (see [Psalm 11:3–7](https://ref.ly/Ps11:3-Ps11:7)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 18:7 (id: 24209)

The destabilization of the whole earth will be a means of God’s judgment ([Psalm 77:17–18](https://ref.ly/Ps77:17-Ps77:18); [96:9](https://ref.ly/Ps96:9); [97:4](https://ref.ly/Ps97:4); [99:1](https://ref.ly/Ps99:1); [104:32](https://ref.ly/Ps104:32); [Isaiah 29:6](https://ref.ly/Isa29:6); [Micah 1:3–4](https://ref.ly/Mic1:3-Mic1:4); [Nahum 1:2–6](https://ref.ly/Nah1:2-Nah1:6); [Haggai 2:7](https://ref.ly/Hag2:7), [21](https://ref.ly/Hag2:21)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Foundation (ID: `realia:Foundation`)

## Psalms 18:7–15 (id: 24208)

The psalmist describes the Lord coming down from heaven to earth. This moment includes powerful signs such as earthquakes, fire, smoke, darkness, and rain. All creation trembles when God appears.

The psalm shows God as the Divine Warrior (a title that describes God fighting for his people, see [Psalm 97:2–5](https://ref.ly/Ps97:2-Ps97:5)) and as the Judge who rules with justice (see [50:1–6](https://ref.ly/Ps50:1-Ps50:6)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:7-15

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Foundation (ID: `realia:Foundation`); Arrow (ID: `keyterm:Arrow`); Winged Creature (ID: `realia:WingedCreature`)

## Psalms 18:8 (id: 24210)

* *His nostrils … His mouth*: The psalmist compares God to an angry person or a fierce creature (see [Job 41:12–22](https://ref.ly/Job41:12-Job41:22)).
* The frightening images of smoke, flames, and glowing coals show God’s anger against sin.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:8


## Psalms 18:9–11 (id: 24211)

The Lord rules over all of nature (see [Psalm 104:2–4](https://ref.ly/Ps104:2-Ps104:4); [148:5–6](https://ref.ly/Ps148:5-Ps148:6)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:9-11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Winged Creature (ID: `realia:WingedCreature`)

*a cherub*: See study notes on 1 Chronicles 28:18; 2 Chronicles 3:10–13.

## Psalms 18:19 (id: 24185)

God loves and delights in the psalmist (see [Psalm 18:1](https://ref.ly/Ps18:1)), so he will save him ([37:23](https://ref.ly/Ps37:23); [41:11](https://ref.ly/Ps41:11); [91:14–16](https://ref.ly/Ps91:14-Ps91:16)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:19


## Psalms 18:20–24 (id: 24187)

The psalmist states that loyalty brings rewards. He trusts in the Lord (see [Psalm 16:1](https://ref.ly/Ps16:1)) and promises to live with integrity (see [Psalms 1](https://ref.ly/Ps1:1-Ps1:6), [15](https://ref.ly/Ps15:1-Ps15:5), [24](https://ref.ly/Ps24:1-Ps24:10); see also [19:12–14](https://ref.ly/Ps19:12-Ps19:14)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:20-24

## Psalms 18:21 (id: 24188)

* *kept the ways of the LORD*: The psalmist follows God's instructions faithfully (see [Psalm 25:8–10](https://ref.ly/Ps25:8-Ps25:10)).
* *have not wickedly departed*: The psalmist avoids foolishness (see [Psalms 1](https://ref.ly/Ps1:1-Ps1:6) and [14](https://ref.ly/Ps14:1-Ps14:7)) and chooses wisdom (see [Psalms 1](https://ref.ly/Ps1:1-Ps1:6) and [15](https://ref.ly/Ps15:1-Ps15:5)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:21

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** To Act Wickedly (ID: `keyterm:ActWickedly`); The Way (ID: `keyterm:TheWay`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 18:25–29 (id: 24189)

The faithful God remains true to his character. He loves faithfulness, innocence, and purity, and he hates wickedness (see [Psalm 1:6](https://ref.ly/Ps1:6)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:25-29

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Complete (ID: `keyterm:Complete`); Loyal Person (ID: `keyterm:LoyalPerson`); To Show Oneself Loyal (ID: `keyterm:ShowOneselfLoyal`); Lamp (ID: `realia:Lamp`)

## Psalms 18:26 (id: 24190)

* The pure have clean hands (see [Psalm 18:20–24](https://ref.ly/Ps18:20-Ps18:24)).
* *to the crooked You show Yourself shrewd*: The Lord understands everyone's character and responds to them fairly.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:26


## Psalms 18:27 (id: 24191)

*haughty*: The Lord hates pride ([Psalm 101:5](https://ref.ly/Ps101:5); [131:1](https://ref.ly/Ps131:1); see [Proverbs 6:16–17](https://ref.ly/Prov6:16-Prov6:17); [21:4](https://ref.ly/Prov21:4); [30:13](https://ref.ly/Prov30:13)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:27


## Psalms 18:28 (id: 24192)

*light my lamp*: The Lord renews the psalmist's life, helping him overcome the darkness of difficult times (see [Psalms 112:4](https://ref.ly/Ps112:4); [119:105](https://ref.ly/Ps119:105)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:28

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Lamp (ID: `realia:Lamp`)

## Psalms 18:29 (id: 24193)

*scale a wall*: God helps his servants fight their battles.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:29

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 18:30 (id: 24196)

* *perfect*: God’s character is full of integrity. He is completely trustworthy (see [Psalm 18:26](https://ref.ly/Ps18:26)).
* God promises to protect his people, and he keeps this promise.
* Seeking God's protection requires trust and faith. Despite his feelings or external circumstances, the psalmist makes a deep commitment to the Lord. He experiences joy ([Psalms 5:11](https://ref.ly/Ps5:11); [64:10](https://ref.ly/Ps64:10)) as he waits for the Lord's rescue ([Psalm 57:1](https://ref.ly/Ps57:1)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:30

The psalmist experiences God’s rescue and provision of victory.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:30-36

The psalmist’s victories come from the Lord. He completely depends on God.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:32-34

## Psalms 18:36 (id: 24198)

A wide path represents freedom and safety (see [Psalms 4:8](https://ref.ly/Ps4:8); [119:35](https://ref.ly/Ps119:35)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:36


## Psalms 18:37–42 (id: 24199)

With help from the Divine Warrior, the psalmist experienced victory over his enemies.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:37-42

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 18:40 (id: 24201)

Placing a foot on the necks of the enemy represents total victory (see [Joshua 10:23–26](https://ref.ly/Josh10:23-Josh10:26)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:40


## Psalms 18:43–45 (id: 24202)

With God’s help and victory, the psalmist gained success in battle and in ruling the nation. In this way, the psalm reflects the promise about David’s royal line in [Psalm 2](https://ref.ly/Ps2:1-Ps2:12). 

This psalm also answers some of the earlier prayers for help. The laments in earlier psalms begin to find resolution here.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:43-45

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Force to Serve (ID: `keyterm:EnticedToServe`)

## Psalms 18:46–50 (id: 24203)

The psalmist again reflects on the Lord’s victories. God’s amazing rescue and the king’s victory are cause for celebration.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:46-50

## Psalms 18:47 (id: 24204)

God defends and vindicates his servant. In this way, God fulfills the mission he gave him ([Psalm 2](https://ref.ly/Ps2:1-Ps2:12); see [47:3](https://ref.ly/Ps47:3); [144:2](https://ref.ly/Ps144:2)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:47

The psalmist's vision of praise *among the nations* inspired Paul in his mission to the Gentiles ([Romans 15:9](https://ref.ly/Rom15:9)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:49

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 18:50 (id: 24206)

God appointed David, his chosen leader, to bring order to God's world (see [Psalm 2](https://ref.ly/Ps2:1-Ps2:12)). Both David and his descendants received this responsibility.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 18:50

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** David (ID: `person:David`); Anointed (ID: `keyterm:Anointed`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`)

## Psalms 19:1 (id: 24217)

* The *glory of God* refers to the Creator's splendor or visible presence ([Psalm 113:4](https://ref.ly/Ps113:4)).
* *God* (Hebrew *’el*): *El* is an ancient name for God. It shows his power as the Creator.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 19:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 19:1–4 (id: 24213)

The created world shows God's wisdom, and the heavens are where God lives ([Psalm 2:4](https://ref.ly/Ps2:4); see [8:1](https://ref.ly/Ps8:1); [57:5](https://ref.ly/Ps57:5); [89:6](https://ref.ly/Ps89:6); [115:3](https://ref.ly/Ps115:3)). From heaven, God watches over people ([Psalms 11:4](https://ref.ly/Ps11:4); [14:2](https://ref.ly/Ps14:2); [33:13](https://ref.ly/Ps33:13); [53:2](https://ref.ly/Ps53:2); [80:14](https://ref.ly/Ps80:14); [102:19](https://ref.ly/Ps102:19)) and helps his followers ([18:6–8](https://ref.ly/Ps18:6-Ps18:8), [13](https://ref.ly/Ps18:13); [20:6](https://ref.ly/Ps20:6); [57:3](https://ref.ly/Ps57:3); [76:8](https://ref.ly/Ps76:8); [144:5](https://ref.ly/Ps144:5)). 

The heavens remain unaffected by human failures and rebellions (see [Psalm 2:1–4](https://ref.ly/Ps2:1-Ps2:4)). They show God's faithfulness, righteousness ([Psalms 36:5](https://ref.ly/Ps36:5); [50:6](https://ref.ly/Ps50:6); [57:10](https://ref.ly/Ps57:10); [85:11](https://ref.ly/Ps85:11); [89:2](https://ref.ly/Ps89:2), [5](https://ref.ly/Ps89:5); [97:6](https://ref.ly/Ps97:6); [108:4](https://ref.ly/Ps108:4)), and wisdom ([136:5](https://ref.ly/Ps136:5); see [119:89–90](https://ref.ly/Ps119:89-Ps119:90)). The heavens reveal the Lord’s glory (see [Psalm 19:1](https://ref.ly/Ps19:1)) and prove his presence and work.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 19:1-4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); Intention (ID: `keyterm:Intention`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 19:1–14 (id: 24212)

God's wisdom is clearly shown in many places:

* It appears in the heavens ([Psalm 19:1–4](https://ref.ly/Ps19:1-Ps19:4)),

* in nature ([Psalm 19:4–6](https://ref.ly/Ps19:4-Ps19:6)),
* in his teachings ([Psalm 19:7–11](https://ref.ly/Ps19:7-Ps19:11)), and
* in the life of the psalmist ([Psalm 19:12–14](https://ref.ly/Ps19:12-Ps19:14))

Like [Psalm 8](https://ref.ly/Ps8:1-Ps8:9), this hymn uses creation to lead people to reflect on God. It offers a calm moment after the intense prayers and victory songs in earlier psalms.

The psalm begins with creation as a source of wisdom. It then moves to God’s word as the perfect source of wisdom. Finally, it ends with the human need for redemption.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 19:1-14

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Complete (ID: `keyterm:Complete`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Intention (ID: `keyterm:Intention`); Instruction (ID: `keyterm:Instruction`); Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); Simple Person (ID: `keyterm:SimplePerson`); Pure (ID: `keyterm:Pure`); Delight (ID: `keyterm:Delight`); Be Blameless (ID: `keyterm:Blameless`); Testimony (ID: `keyterm:Witness.2`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Pronounce Innocent (ID: `keyterm:PronounceInnocent`); Well-Established (ID: `keyterm:BeFirm`); Be (Or Show Oneself) Just (ID: `keyterm:Just`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); Perceive (ID: `keyterm:Perceive`)

## Psalms 19:3–4 (id: 24218)

* God’s wisdom does not need to be spoken. Through creation, it is seen, felt, and experienced. Paul applied this text to condemn Israel for its lack of receptivity to Jesus Christ ([Romans 10:18](https://ref.ly/Rom10:18)).
* The sun is part of God’s creation, but it is not a god, as people of other nations in the ancient Near East commonly believed.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 19:3-4


## Psalms 19:7–11 (id: 24219)

God's instructions to humans show his character and wisdom. His guidance is valuable and enjoyable ([Psalm 119:103](https://ref.ly/Ps119:103)). It nurtures the individual ([Proverbs 16:24](https://ref.ly/Prov16:24); [24:14](https://ref.ly/Prov24:14)) and holds more value than material possessions ([Psalm 81:13–16](https://ref.ly/Ps81:13-Ps81:16)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 19:7-11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Complete (ID: `keyterm:Complete`); Instruction (ID: `keyterm:Instruction`); Pure (ID: `keyterm:Pure`); Simple Person (ID: `keyterm:SimplePerson`); Testimony (ID: `keyterm:Witness.2`); Well-Established (ID: `keyterm:BeFirm`); Be (Or Show Oneself) Just (ID: `keyterm:Just`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`)

## Psalms 19:12–14 (id: 24214)

The psalmist knows his weaknesses, so he prays to remain faithful and commits himself to the Lord.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 19:12-14

The community prays for the king’s protection and victory.

## Psalms 20:1–9 (id: 24236)

This royal prayer asks God to give victory to the king ([Psalm 20:1–5](https://ref.ly/Ps20:1-Ps20:5)). It then expresses confidence that God will bring rescue ([20:6–8](https://ref.ly/Ps20:6-Ps20:8)). The psalm ends with the people asking God to answer their prayer ([20:9](https://ref.ly/Ps20:9)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 20:1-9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Counsel (ID: `keyterm:Counsel`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); Anointed (ID: `keyterm:Anointed`); Horse (ID: `fauna:Horse`); Chariot (ID: `realia:Chariot`); Standard (ID: `realia:Standard`)

## Psalms 20:1–24.10 (id: 24234)

This series of psalms shows a journey from confidence and commitment ([Psalms 20–21](https://ref.ly/Ps20:1-Ps21:13)), through anguish and abandonment ([Psalm 22](https://ref.ly/Ps22:1-Ps22:31)), and finally to comfort, insight, and hope ([Psalms 23–24](https://ref.ly/Ps23:1-Ps24:10)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 20:1-24:10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Psalm (ID: `keyterm:Psalm`); David (ID: `person:David`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Lion (ID: `fauna:Lion`); Bring Honor and Glory on Oneself (ID: `keyterm:BringHonorAndGloryOnOneself`); Poor (ID: `keyterm:Poor`); Lot (ID: `keyterm:Lot`); Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); Plan (ID: `keyterm:Plan`); Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); Strength (ID: `keyterm:Strength`); Deceit (ID: `keyterm:Deceit`); Exempt (ID: `keyterm:Exempt`); Swear (ID: `keyterm:Swear`); Force to Serve (ID: `keyterm:EnticedToServe`); Vow (ID: `keyterm:Vow`); Counsel (ID: `keyterm:Counsel`); Anointed (ID: `keyterm:Anointed`); Majesty (ID: `keyterm:Majesty`); Bashan (ID: `place:Bashan`); Dog (ID: `fauna:Dog`); Sheep (ID: `fauna:Lamb`)

The king returned victorious from battle and received honor from the people. His greatest honor came from God, who gave him blessings, life, and his presence.

## Psalms 21:4 (id: 24250)

God offers a rich quality of life ([Psalms 61:6–7](https://ref.ly/Ps61:6-Ps61:7); [91:14–16](https://ref.ly/Ps91:14-Ps91:16); [128:4–6](https://ref.ly/Ps128:4-Ps128:6)). This life is characterized by:

* God's presence ([Psalms 16:11](https://ref.ly/Ps16:11); [23:6](https://ref.ly/Ps23:6); [27:4](https://ref.ly/Ps27:4); [36:9](https://ref.ly/Ps36:9)),
* joy ([Psalm 16:11](https://ref.ly/Ps16:11))
* goodness ([Psalm 23:6](https://ref.ly/Ps23:6); [34:14–15](https://ref.ly/Ps34:14-Ps34:15)), and
* protection ([Psalm 41:2](https://ref.ly/Ps41:2); [61:7](https://ref.ly/Ps61:7)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 21:4


## Psalms 21:6 (id: 24251)

God's eternal blessings also bring a full life now (see [Psalm 133](https://ref.ly/Ps133:1-Ps133:3)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 21:6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`)

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 21:7

## Psalms 21:8–12 (id: 24253)

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Plan (ID: `keyterm:Plan`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Rope (ID: `realia:Rope`)

## Psalms 21:9 (id: 24254)

The *fiery furnace* and *fire* represent the seriousness of God's judgment on the wicked. In the Old Testament, fire often represents God’s anger against sin and destructive judgment ([Psalms 11:6](https://ref.ly/Ps11:6); [18:8](https://ref.ly/Ps18:8); [50:3](https://ref.ly/Ps50:3); [68:2](https://ref.ly/Ps68:2); [78:21](https://ref.ly/Ps78:21); [79:5](https://ref.ly/Ps79:5); [80:16](https://ref.ly/Ps80:16); [89:46](https://ref.ly/Ps89:46); [97:3](https://ref.ly/Ps97:3); [104:4](https://ref.ly/Ps104:4); [140:10](https://ref.ly/Ps140:10); see [Isaiah 66:24](https://ref.ly/Isa66:24)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 21:9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

*descendants … offspring*: Evil will end when God destroys all opposition to himself ([Psalm 109:13](https://ref.ly/Ps109:13); compare [18:50](https://ref.ly/Ps18:50); [22:30–31](https://ref.ly/Ps22:30-Ps22:31); [25:13](https://ref.ly/Ps25:13); [102:28](https://ref.ly/Ps102:28)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 21:10

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 21:13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

The psalmist switches between thinking about his current difficult situation and remembering the Lord's past faithfulness. He ends the psalm with a prayer for rescue.

## Psalms 22:1–31 (id: 24257)

All previous laments seem minor compared to this outcry against the enemy and God's abandonment. The psalm has two main sections: 

* the lament ([Psalm 22:1–21](https://ref.ly/Ps22:1-Ps22:21)) and
* praise for redemption ([Psalm 22:22–31](https://ref.ly/Ps22:22-Ps22:31)).

This naturally leads to [Psalm 23](https://ref.ly/Ps23:1-Ps23:6). 

The apostles saw this psalm as expressing the sufferings of Jesus Christ. He ultimately fulfilled the purpose of David's humiliation, rejection by people, and divine abandonment ([Matthew 27:35](https://ref.ly/Matt27:35), [39](https://ref.ly/Matt27:39), [43](https://ref.ly/Matt27:43), [46](https://ref.ly/Matt27:46); [Mark 15:34](https://ref.ly/Mark15:34); [John 19:23–24](https://ref.ly/John19:23-John19:24), [28](https://ref.ly/John19:28); [Hebrews 2:12](https://ref.ly/Heb2:12)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 22:1-31

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Lion (ID: `fauna:Lion`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Dog (ID: `fauna:Dog`); Bring Honor and Glory on Oneself (ID: `keyterm:BringHonorAndGloryOnOneself`); Poor (ID: `keyterm:Poor`); Lot (ID: `keyterm:Lot`); Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Strength (ID: `keyterm:Strength`); Force to Serve (ID: `keyterm:EnticedToServe`); Vow (ID: `keyterm:Vow`); Bashan (ID: `place:Bashan`); Wild Ox (ID: `fauna:WildOx`); Sheep (ID: `fauna:Lamb`); Cattle (ID: `fauna:Bull`); Worm (ID: `fauna:Maggot`)

The psalmist thinks about his past relationship with God (see [Psalm 22:3–5](https://ref.ly/Ps22:3-Ps22:5)). The Lord has been his very life.

Bashan*,* a region famous for its herds and plants, produced strong bulls ([Psalm 68:30](https://ref.ly/Ps68:30)).

The psalmist again shares his deep pain and suffering (see [Psalm 22:1–2](https://ref.ly/Ps22:1-Ps22:2), [6–8](https://ref.ly/Ps22:6-Ps22:8)). His trouble comes from human beings who act like wild animals.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 22:12-18

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Lion (ID: `fauna:Lion`); Lot (ID: `keyterm:Lot`); Strength (ID: `keyterm:Strength`); Bashan (ID: `place:Bashan`); Cattle (ID: `fauna:Bull`); Dog (ID: `fauna:Dog`)

## Psalms 22:14 (id: 24260)

The psalmist feels like he is *poured out like water*. This describes a temporary and empty life that leaves him drained.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 22:14


## Psalms 22:15 (id: 24261)

* Facing adversity is as terrible as dying of thirst (see [Psalm 137:6](https://ref.ly/Ps137:6)).
* Jesus might have thought of this verse when he said, “I am thirsty” ([John 19:28](https://ref.ly/John19:28)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 22:15

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Strength (ID: `keyterm:Strength`)

## Psalms 22:16 (id: 24262)

The wild pack of dogs represents aggressive and dishonest people.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 22:16

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Lion (ID: `fauna:Lion`); Dog (ID: `fauna:Dog`)

## Psalms 22:17–18 (id: 24263)

* *I can count all my bones*: The psalmist's enemies have shamed him by taking his clothes. Jesus also faced this humiliation (see [Matthew 27:35](https://ref.ly/Matt27:35)).
* *stare and gloat over me*: Enemies make the psalmist a target of their ridicule (see [Psalms 35:24](https://ref.ly/Ps35:24), [26](https://ref.ly/Ps35:26); [38:16](https://ref.ly/Ps38:16); [55:3](https://ref.ly/Ps55:3); compare [59:10](https://ref.ly/Ps59:10)).
* *cast lots*: Casting lots was sometimes a way for religious people to seek God's guidance ([Leviticus 16:8](https://ref.ly/Lev16:8); [Numbers 27:21](https://ref.ly/Num27:21); [Acts 1:24–26](https://ref.ly/Acts1:24-Acts1:26)). Here, the lots are thrown as a game of chance. The soldiers threw dice for the clothing of Jesus after the crucifixion ([Matthew 27:35](https://ref.ly/Matt27:35); [Mark 15:24](https://ref.ly/Mark15:24); [Luke 23:34](https://ref.ly/Luke23:34); [John 19:24](https://ref.ly/John19:24)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 22:17-18

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Lot (ID: `keyterm:Lot`)

The psalmist prays to God for help with his troubles. Only the Lord can protect him from dogs, lions, and oxen.

The Lord invites everyone, both rich and poor ([Psalm 22:26](https://ref.ly/Ps22:26)), to the banquet as long as they worship him.

## Psalms 22:30 (id: 24276)

Future generations will join those from the past in praising the Lord ([Psalm 22:3–5](https://ref.ly/Ps22:3-Ps22:5)). The descendants of the godly will receive blessings ([Psalm 22:30–31](https://ref.ly/Ps22:30-Ps22:31)), unlike the children of the wicked ([21:10](https://ref.ly/Ps21:10)).

## Psalms 22:31 (id: 24277)

The Lord's acts of redemption ([Psalms 40:9](https://ref.ly/Ps40:9); [50:6](https://ref.ly/Ps50:6); [71:15](https://ref.ly/Ps71:15), [16](https://ref.ly/Ps71:16), [24](https://ref.ly/Ps71:24); [97:6](https://ref.ly/Ps97:6)) show that he is righteous (see [4:1](https://ref.ly/Ps4:1)). His righteousness gives him the character needed for leadership, making fair decisions, and building relationships. This virtue brings harmony and order (see [Psalm 50:6](https://ref.ly/Ps50:6)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 22:31


## Psalms 23:1 (id: 24286)

The Lord promises to care for his children ([Psalm 34:9–10](https://ref.ly/Ps34:9-Ps34:10); [Isaiah 51:14](https://ref.ly/Isa51:14)). He showed this ability to Israel in the wilderness ([Deuteronomy 2:7](https://ref.ly/Deut2:7)) and in the promised land ([Deuteronomy 8:9](https://ref.ly/Deut8:9)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 23:1

## Psalms 23:1–6 (id: 24285)

This psalm, which expresses trust and confidence in the Lord, has been a favorite of God's people for generations. It assures us of God's presence during difficult times. It brings to mind images of the Good Shepherd ([Psalm 23:1–3](https://ref.ly/Ps23:1-Ps23:3); see [Ezekiel 34:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:12); [John 10:11](https://ref.ly/John10:11)) and the messianic banquet ([Psalm 23:5–6](https://ref.ly/Ps23:5-Ps23:6); see [Isaiah 25:6](https://ref.ly/Isa25:6); [Revelation 19:9](https://ref.ly/Rev19:9)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 23:1-6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Psalm (ID: `keyterm:Psalm`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); David (ID: `person:David`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Staff (ID: `realia:Staff`); Anointing Oil (ID: `realia:AnointingOil`); Sheep (ID: `fauna:Lamb`); House (ID: `realia:House`); Green Vegetation (ID: `flora:GreenVegetation`); Rod (ID: `realia:Rod`); Olive Oil (ID: `realia:OliveOil`); Flock (ID: `fauna:Flock`); Grass (ID: `flora:Grass`); Goat (ID: `fauna:Goat`)

## Psalms 23:1–28.9 (id: 24283)

These psalms develop the importance of: 

* integrity ([Psalms 25:4–7](https://ref.ly/Ps25:4-Ps25:7), [12–14](https://ref.ly/Ps25:12-Ps25:14), [21](https://ref.ly/Ps25:21); [26:6](https://ref.ly/Ps26:6), [11](https://ref.ly/Ps26:11); [27:7–12](https://ref.ly/Ps27:7-Ps27:12)),
* God's care as a shepherd ([Psalms 23:1](https://ref.ly/Ps23:1); [28:9](https://ref.ly/Ps28:9)),
* God's guidance ([Psalms 23:2–3](https://ref.ly/Ps23:2-Ps23:3); [25:5](https://ref.ly/Ps25:5); [27:11](https://ref.ly/Ps27:11)),
* God's goodness ([Psalms 23:6](https://ref.ly/Ps23:6); [25:7–8](https://ref.ly/Ps25:7-Ps25:8); [27:13](https://ref.ly/Ps27:13)), and
* living in his house ([Psalms 23:6](https://ref.ly/Ps23:6); [26:8](https://ref.ly/Ps26:8); [27:4](https://ref.ly/Ps27:4), [8](https://ref.ly/Ps27:8)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 23:1-28:9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); David (ID: `person:David`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); To Sin (ID: `keyterm:Sin`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); To Be Kindly Disposed (ID: `keyterm:KindlyDisposed`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); Psalm (ID: `keyterm:Psalm`); Covenant (ID: `keyterm:Covenant`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); The Way (ID: `keyterm:TheWay`); Forgive (ID: `keyterm:Forgive`); Redeem (ID: `keyterm:Redeem`); Plea for Mercy (ID: `keyterm:Supplication`); House (ID: `realia:House`); Innocence (ID: `keyterm:Innocence`); Believe In (ID: `keyterm:BelieveIn`); Shield (ID: `keyterm:Shield`); Precept (ID: `keyterm:Precept`); Straightness (ID: `keyterm:Straightness`); Slaughtering (ID: `keyterm:Slaughtering`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Poor (ID: `keyterm:Poor`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`)

## Psalms 23:2 (id: 24287)

* Sheep rest after they have eaten enough ([Zephaniah 3:13](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:13); see [Isaiah 11:7](https://ref.ly/Isa11:7); compare [Isaiah 13:20](https://ref.ly/Isa13:20); [27:10](https://ref.ly/Isa27:10)).
* The Lord guides the sheep for their benefit (see [Psalm 5:8](https://ref.ly/Ps5:8)).
* Sheep drink only from still water, so the shepherd finds peaceful streams for them.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 23:2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Green Vegetation (ID: `flora:GreenVegetation`); Grass (ID: `flora:Grass`)

## Psalms 23:3 (id: 24288)

* Compare [Psalm 19:7](https://ref.ly/Ps19:7); *The Law of the LORD is perfect, reviving the soul*.
* The Lord guides by:

    + rescuing his people from evil ([Psalm 23:4–5](https://ref.ly/Ps23:4-Ps23:5); see [31:3](https://ref.ly/Ps31:3)),
        + bringing them back to himself ([Psalms 5:8](https://ref.ly/Ps5:8); [61:2](https://ref.ly/Ps61:2)),
        + caring for his people ([Psalms 48:14](https://ref.ly/Ps48:14); [139:10](https://ref.ly/Ps139:10)),
        + ruling his people ([Psalm 67:4](https://ref.ly/Ps67:4)),
        + counseling his people ([Psalm 73:24](https://ref.ly/Ps73:24)),
        + protecting his people ([Psalms 78:53](https://ref.ly/Ps78:53); [107:30](https://ref.ly/Ps107:30); [143:11](https://ref.ly/Ps143:11)), and
        + instructing them in wisdom ([Psalm 27:11](https://ref.ly/Ps27:11); see [Proverbs 4:11](https://ref.ly/Prov4:11)).
* The Lord’s name guarantees the relationship. He protects his reputation against any claims of abandoning his people or allowing them to sin (see [Psalms 25:11](https://ref.ly/Ps25:11); [31:3](https://ref.ly/Ps31:3); [106:8](https://ref.ly/Ps106:8); [143:11](https://ref.ly/Ps143:11)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 23:3

## Psalms 23:5 (id: 24290)

* A feast shows the Lord’s care and mercy for his people (see [Psalm 22:26](https://ref.ly/Ps22:26), [29](https://ref.ly/Ps22:29)). Enemies may watch, but they cannot disturb the guests.
* The ritual of anointing the head with oil honored guests ([Psalm 92:9–11](https://ref.ly/Ps92:9-Ps92:11)), and provided hospitality and refreshment ([104:15](https://ref.ly/Ps104:15); [133:2](https://ref.ly/Ps133:2)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 23:5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Anointing Oil (ID: `realia:AnointingOil`); Olive Oil (ID: `realia:OliveOil`)

## Psalms 23:6 (id: 24291)

The Lord shows his unfailing love through his presence and the unique benefits he provides ([Psalms 69:16](https://ref.ly/Ps69:16); [86:17](https://ref.ly/Ps86:17); [109:21](https://ref.ly/Ps109:21); see [18:6](https://ref.ly/Ps18:6); [61:4](https://ref.ly/Ps61:4)). He gives care that surpasses the temporary challenges from enemies ([Psalm 7:1](https://ref.ly/Ps7:1)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 23:6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); House (ID: `realia:House`)

## Psalms 25:1–22 (id: 24300)

This personal lament includes wisdom teachings and a community lament. It starts and ends with a declaration of trust in the Lord ([Psalm 25:1–3](https://ref.ly/Ps25:1-Ps25:3), [15–22](https://ref.ly/Ps25:15-Ps25:22)). The psalmist prays for guidance ([Psalm 25:4–7](https://ref.ly/Ps25:4-Ps25:7)) and urges the godly to live wisely ([25:12–14](https://ref.ly/Ps25:12-Ps25:14)). 

In addition to the enemies who trouble him, the psalmist confesses his sins([Psalm 25:7](https://ref.ly/Ps25:7), [11](https://ref.ly/Ps25:11), [18](https://ref.ly/Ps25:18)) as part of his distress. Trust grows by understanding God's nature: He is compassionate, faithful, and good ([Psalm 25:8–10](https://ref.ly/Ps25:8-Ps25:10)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 25:1-22

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); To Sin (ID: `keyterm:Sin`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Covenant (ID: `keyterm:Covenant`); Forgive (ID: `keyterm:Forgive`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); Precept (ID: `keyterm:Precept`); To Be Kindly Disposed (ID: `keyterm:KindlyDisposed`); Straightness (ID: `keyterm:Straightness`); David (ID: `person:David`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Poor (ID: `keyterm:Poor`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); The Way (ID: `keyterm:TheWay`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Redeem (ID: `keyterm:Redeem`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`); Dispossess (ID: `keyterm:Dispossess`); Net (ID: `realia:Net`); Casting Net (ID: `realia:CastingNet`)

The psalmist asks for forgiveness for his many sins ([Psalms 25:7](https://ref.ly/Ps25:7); [32:1–5](https://ref.ly/Ps32:1-Ps32:5)). The sins of Israel’s kings showed the weaknesses in David’s family line and why it failed in Old Testament Israel (see [Psalm 89](https://ref.ly/Ps89:1-Ps89:52)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 25:11

## Psalms 25:13 (id: 24304)

* *prosperity*: See [Psalm 23:6](https://ref.ly/Ps23:6).
