* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 27:1 (id: 24329)

Light shows the Lord's goodness (P[salm 27:13](https://ref.ly/Ps27:13); see [4:6](https://ref.ly/Ps4:6); [23:6](https://ref.ly/Ps23:6); [31:19](https://ref.ly/Ps31:19); [38:10](https://ref.ly/Ps38:10); [43:3](https://ref.ly/Ps43:3); [56:13](https://ref.ly/Ps56:13)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 27:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); David (ID: `person:David`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Fortress (ID: `realia:Fortress`)

## Psalms 27:1–3 (id: 24327)

The psalmist acknowledges the Lord at the center of his life ([Psalm 27:1](https://ref.ly/Ps27:1)). Because of God’s presence, he engages his adversaries with confidence. They cannot intimidate him or disrupt his inner peace.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 27:1-3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); David (ID: `person:David`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Fortress (ID: `realia:Fortress`)

## Psalms 27:1–14 (id: 24326)

This psalm combines two types—a psalm of confidence ([Psalm 27:1–6](https://ref.ly/Ps27:1-Ps27:6)) and an individual lament ([27:7–14](https://ref.ly/Ps27:7-Ps27:14))—united by the theme of longing for the Lord's presence. The psalmist focuses on the Lord, making his own circumstances less important. The psalmist shows strong faith by being open to the Lord's guidance, timing, and priorities.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 27:1-14

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Believe In (ID: `keyterm:BelieveIn`); Slaughtering (ID: `keyterm:Slaughtering`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); The Way (ID: `keyterm:TheWay`); David (ID: `person:David`); To Be Kindly Disposed (ID: `keyterm:KindlyDisposed`); House (ID: `realia:House`); Fortress (ID: `realia:Fortress`)

## Psalms 27:2 (id: 24330)

The psalmist's enemies want to take advantage of him and live off his resources (see study note on Psalm 14:4).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 27:2
* **Associated Articles:** Psalms 14:4 (ID: `24087@TyndaleStudyNotes`)


## Psalms 27:3 (id: 24331)

*keep my trust*: See [Psalms 25:2](https://ref.ly/Ps25:2); [26:1](https://ref.ly/Ps26:1).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 27:3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`)

## Psalms 27:4–6 (id: 24332)

Seeking and enjoying the Lord's presence gives the psalmist confidence and security. He trusts in God's protection and looks forward to giving thanks and praise (see [Psalm 18:6](https://ref.ly/Ps18:6)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 27:4-6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Slaughtering (ID: `keyterm:Slaughtering`); House (ID: `realia:House`)

## Psalms 27:5 (id: 24333)

*tent* (or *tabernacle*): This early shrine was where the Israelites worshipped before they built the temple.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 27:5


## Psalms 27:6 (id: 24334)

* *my head will be held high*: This is a sign of victory.
* The granting of the request of [Psalm 27:4](https://ref.ly/Ps27:4) leads to sacrifices with joyful shouts.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 27:6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Slaughtering (ID: `keyterm:Slaughtering`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 27:7–8 (id: 24336)

Unlike the wicked ([Psalm 10:4](https://ref.ly/Ps10:4)), who only want a temporary advantage ([78:34–36](https://ref.ly/Ps78:34-Ps78:36)), the godly search for the Lord like a thirsty person seeks water in the desert ([63:1](https://ref.ly/Ps63:1); [105:4](https://ref.ly/Ps105:4); [119:2](https://ref.ly/Ps119:2), [10](https://ref.ly/Ps119:10); [Matthew 6:33](https://ref.ly/Matt6:33)). They act wisely ([Psalm 34:10](https://ref.ly/Ps34:10), [14](https://ref.ly/Ps34:14)) while waiting for the Lord to resolve their crisis ([34:4](https://ref.ly/Ps34:4); [69:6](https://ref.ly/Ps69:6); [77:2](https://ref.ly/Ps77:2); [119:58](https://ref.ly/Ps119:58)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 27:7-8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); To Be Kindly Disposed (ID: `keyterm:KindlyDisposed`)

## Psalms 27:7–12 (id: 24335)

The psalmist asks for the Lord's mercy, presence, and guidance. For the psalmist, God is the greatest good, while enemies and other situations are less important.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 27:7-12

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); To Be Kindly Disposed (ID: `keyterm:KindlyDisposed`); The Way (ID: `keyterm:TheWay`)

## Psalms 27:13–14 (id: 24328)

The psalmist shows confidence and hope in the Lord's presence. Living in faith means waiting patiently, knowing the Lord is good. This life is not always easy for the faithful ([Psalm 42:2–3](https://ref.ly/Ps42:2-Ps42:3); [43:5](https://ref.ly/Ps43:5)), but their future is secure ([37:9](https://ref.ly/Ps37:9); [130:7](https://ref.ly/Ps130:7); see [9:18](https://ref.ly/Ps9:18)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 27:13-14

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Believe In (ID: `keyterm:BelieveIn`)

## Psalms 28:1 (id: 24339)

*if You remain silent*: The psalmist asks the Lord to act ([Psalm 35:22](https://ref.ly/Ps35:22); [50:3](https://ref.ly/Ps50:3); [83:1](https://ref.ly/Ps83:1); [109:1](https://ref.ly/Ps109:1)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 28:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** David (ID: `person:David`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 28:1–2 (id: 24337)

The psalmist asks the Lord to listen. His urgent request comes from the tension between his close relationship with the Lord and the trouble he is facing.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 28:1-2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** David (ID: `person:David`); Plea for Mercy (ID: `keyterm:Supplication`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 28:1–9 (id: 24338)

In this personal lament, the psalmist asks for justice and mercy. He trusts in the Lord's strength and faithfulness and prays for the Lord's people.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 28:1-9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Plea for Mercy (ID: `keyterm:Supplication`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Shield (ID: `keyterm:Shield`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); David (ID: `person:David`); Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); To Cause to Possess (ID: `keyterm:Possess`); Anointed (ID: `keyterm:Anointed`); Perceive (ID: `keyterm:Perceive`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Sheep (ID: `fauna:Lamb`); Small Shield (ID: `realia:SmallShield`); Fortress (ID: `realia:Fortress`)

## Psalms 28:2 (id: 24340)

People often raised their hands as a sign of devoted prayer ([Psalms 63:4](https://ref.ly/Ps63:4); [134:2](https://ref.ly/Ps134:2)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 28:2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Plea for Mercy (ID: `keyterm:Supplication`)

## Psalms 28:3–5 (id: 24341)

The psalmist asks God to punish his enemies and prove his innocence.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 28:3-5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); Perceive (ID: `keyterm:Perceive`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 28:5 (id: 24342)

The wicked do not acknowledge the difference between the sacred and the common, so they have no respect for the Lord’s creation, redemption, or revelation.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 28:5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Perceive (ID: `keyterm:Perceive`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 28:6–8 (id: 24343)

The psalmist anticipates rescue because of his confidence in the Lord’s justice.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 28:6-8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Shield (ID: `keyterm:Shield`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Plea for Mercy (ID: `keyterm:Supplication`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Anointed (ID: `keyterm:Anointed`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Fortress (ID: `realia:Fortress`); Small Shield (ID: `realia:SmallShield`)

## Psalms 28:7 (id: 24344)

*my strength and my shield*: The Lord protects the psalmist (see [Psalm 59:9](https://ref.ly/Ps59:9), [17](https://ref.ly/Ps59:17)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 28:7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Shield (ID: `keyterm:Shield`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Small Shield (ID: `realia:SmallShield`)

## Psalms 28:9 (id: 24345)

* The poem closes with intercession for the Lord’s people.
* *bless Your inheritance*: See [Psalms 29:11](https://ref.ly/Ps29:11); [133:3](https://ref.ly/Ps133:3).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 28:9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); To Cause to Possess (ID: `keyterm:Possess`); Sheep (ID: `fauna:Lamb`)

## Psalms 29:1–2 (id: 24347)

* The psalmist issues a call to honor the Lord for his power (see [Psalm 96:7–8](https://ref.ly/Ps96:7-Ps96:8)).
* *O heavenly beings*: Angels or deities who, some believed, held special powers. The psalmist did not believe in other gods but called on people who thought they were real to look to the Lord as the only God.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 29:1-2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); David (ID: `person:David`); Psalm (ID: `keyterm:Psalm`)

## Psalms 29:1–11 (id: 24346)

This nature hymn in praise of the Creator declares that the Lord holds all power over nature. God uses the same unsurpassed strength to care for his people.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 29:1-11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); Lebanon (ID: `place:Lebanon`); Strength (ID: `keyterm:Strength`); Psalm (ID: `keyterm:Psalm`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); David (ID: `person:David`); A Group of People (ID: `group:GenericGroup`); Mount Hermon (ID: `place:HermonMount`); Kadesh-barnea (ID: `place:Kadesh-barnea`); Molten Image (ID: `realia:MoltenImage`); Deer (ID: `fauna:Deer`); Cattle (ID: `fauna:Bull`); Cedar (ID: `flora:Cedar`); Wild Ox (ID: `fauna:WildOx`); Sandalwood (ID: `flora:Sandalwood`)

## Psalms 29:3–9 (id: 24350)

The Hebrew word *qol* can mean "voice," "noise" ([1 Kings 1:45](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:45)), or "thunder" ([Joel 3:16](https://ref.ly/Joel3:16)). The Lord's voice is awe\-inspiring and overpowers any sounds people might attribute to other gods.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 29:3-9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); Lebanon (ID: `place:Lebanon`); Strength (ID: `keyterm:Strength`); A Group of People (ID: `group:GenericGroup`); Mount Hermon (ID: `place:HermonMount`); Kadesh-barnea (ID: `place:Kadesh-barnea`); Molten Image (ID: `realia:MoltenImage`); Deer (ID: `fauna:Deer`); Cattle (ID: `fauna:Bull`); Cedar (ID: `flora:Cedar`); Wild Ox (ID: `fauna:WildOx`); Sandalwood (ID: `flora:Sandalwood`)

## Psalms 29:5 (id: 24351)

The cedars of Lebanon were famous for their large size and high quality (see [Isaiah 2:13](https://ref.ly/Isa2:13)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 29:5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Lebanon (ID: `place:Lebanon`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Cedar (ID: `flora:Cedar`)

## Psalms 29:6 (id: 24352)

* Lebanon’s mountains and Sirion (another name for Mount Hermon) are high mountains to the north of Israel.
* The Lord can make even huge mountains shake when he commands it (see [Psalm 114:4](https://ref.ly/Ps114:4)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 29:6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Lebanon (ID: `place:Lebanon`); Mount Hermon (ID: `place:HermonMount`); Molten Image (ID: `realia:MoltenImage`); Cattle (ID: `fauna:Bull`); Wild Ox (ID: `fauna:WildOx`)

## Psalms 29:8 (id: 24353)

The Wilderness of Kadesh was south of Judah. People could hear God's voice across the land, from the north ([Psalm 29:6](https://ref.ly/Ps29:6)) to the south.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 29:8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Kadesh-barnea (ID: `place:Kadesh-barnea`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 29:10 (id: 24348)

The image of strong floodwaters relates to the great flood ([Genesis 6:17](https://ref.ly/Gen6:17)). The Lord rules as king over all creation. No one challenges his kingdom.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 29:10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 29:11 (id: 24349)

The ruler of nature blesses his people with peace (see [Psalms 28:8–9](https://ref.ly/Ps28:8-Ps28:9); [37:11](https://ref.ly/Ps37:11); [133:3](https://ref.ly/Ps133:3)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 29:11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 30:1 (id: 24365)

* *I will exalt You* is a call to praise and thank the Lord ([Psalms 34:3](https://ref.ly/Ps34:3); [81:1](https://ref.ly/Ps81:1); [99:5](https://ref.ly/Ps99:5), [9](https://ref.ly/Ps99:9); [107:32](https://ref.ly/Ps107:32); [118:28](https://ref.ly/Ps118:28); [145:1](https://ref.ly/Ps145:1); see [Exodus 15:1](https://ref.ly/Exod15:1); [Isaiah 25:1](https://ref.ly/Isa25:1)).
* *You have lifted me up*: God saved the psalmist from death ([Psalm 30:3](https://ref.ly/Ps30:3)).
* God, in his justice and care, did not let the psalmist’s enemies win (see [Psalms 26:9](https://ref.ly/Ps26:9); [27:12](https://ref.ly/Ps27:12); [28:3](https://ref.ly/Ps28:3); [31:8](https://ref.ly/Ps31:8)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 30:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 30:1–3 (id: 24364)

The psalmist praises God for his victory over difficult circumstances, comparing it to being saved from death.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 30:1-3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Hell (ID: `place:Hell`)

## Psalms 30:1–12 (id: 24363)

This individual thanksgiving psalm begins by praising the Lord for his salvation ([Psalm 30:1–3](https://ref.ly/Ps30:1-Ps30:3)). It then contrasts God's favor with his anger ([Psalm 30:4–7](https://ref.ly/Ps30:4-Ps30:7)) and ends with a transformation from mourning to dancing ([30:8–12](https://ref.ly/Ps30:8-Ps30:12)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 30:1-12

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Delight (ID: `keyterm:Delight`); Hell (ID: `place:Hell`); Round Dance (ID: `keyterm:RoundDance`); To Beg for Mercy (ID: `keyterm:BegForMercy`); Loyal Person (ID: `keyterm:LoyalPerson`); To Be Kindly Disposed (ID: `keyterm:KindlyDisposed`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); Sackcloth (ID: `realia:Sackcloth`)

## Psalms 30:title (id: 24369)

Perhaps David wrote this psalm before the dedication of the temple, making his own experience representative of the nation’s.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 30:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Delight (ID: `keyterm:Delight`); Hell (ID: `place:Hell`); Round Dance (ID: `keyterm:RoundDance`); To Beg for Mercy (ID: `keyterm:BegForMercy`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); Loyal Person (ID: `keyterm:LoyalPerson`); To Be Kindly Disposed (ID: `keyterm:KindlyDisposed`); Sackcloth (ID: `realia:Sackcloth`)

## Psalms 30:4 (id: 24366)

The godly should celebrate the Lord’s acts of rescue with song and praise ([Psalms 33:21](https://ref.ly/Ps33:21); [97:12](https://ref.ly/Ps97:12); [103:1](https://ref.ly/Ps103:1); [105:3](https://ref.ly/Ps105:3); [145:21](https://ref.ly/Ps145:21)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 30:4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Loyal Person (ID: `keyterm:LoyalPerson`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 30:6–7 (id: 24367)

* *I will never be shaken*: The psalmist admits his overconfident attitude (compare [Psalm 32:3–4](https://ref.ly/Ps32:3-Ps32:4)).
* *my mountain stand strong*: In the psalms, this phrase powerfully symbolizes stability and God's control (see [Psalms 11:1](https://ref.ly/Ps11:1); [18:7](https://ref.ly/Ps18:7); [36:6](https://ref.ly/Ps36:6); [46:2](https://ref.ly/Ps46:2); [72:3](https://ref.ly/Ps72:3); [76:4](https://ref.ly/Ps76:4); [83:14](https://ref.ly/Ps83:14); [97:5](https://ref.ly/Ps97:5); [114:4](https://ref.ly/Ps114:4), [6](https://ref.ly/Ps114:6); [125:2](https://ref.ly/Ps125:2)).
* *I was dismayed*: The absence of God’s favor destroys the security of the proud (compare [Psalm 104:27–30](https://ref.ly/Ps104:27-Ps104:30)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 30:6-7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Delight (ID: `keyterm:Delight`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 30:8–12 (id: 24368)

The psalmist faced death (also in [Psalm 30:1–3](https://ref.ly/Ps30:1-Ps30:3)), either literally or figuratively. When he turned to the Lord, the Lord rescued him and changed his mourning into joyful dancing. Only God's favor can permanently and powerfully overcome human failure and deep despair.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 30:8-12

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Round Dance (ID: `keyterm:RoundDance`); To Beg for Mercy (ID: `keyterm:BegForMercy`); Hell (ID: `place:Hell`); To Be Kindly Disposed (ID: `keyterm:KindlyDisposed`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); Sackcloth (ID: `realia:Sackcloth`)

## Psalms 31:1–5 (id: 24371)

The Lord provides safety to anyone seeking his protection. The psalmist trusts and submits to the Lord. See also [Psalm 71:1–3](https://ref.ly/Ps71:1-Ps71:3).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 31:1-5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Fortress (ID: `realia:Fortress`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`); Redeem (ID: `keyterm:Redeem`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); House (ID: `realia:House`); Casting Net (ID: `realia:CastingNet`); Net (ID: `realia:Net`)

## Psalms 31:1–24 (id: 24370)

The psalmist urges the faithful to seek safety in the Lord and wait for his salvation. After experiencing something like death, he shares how the Lord remained faithful. This psalm points ahead to the suffering of Jesus ([Luke 23:46](https://ref.ly/Luke23:46)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 31:1-24

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Fortress (ID: `realia:Fortress`); Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Loyal Person (ID: `keyterm:LoyalPerson`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); Under Siege (ID: `keyterm:StateOfBeingUnderSiege`); Strength (ID: `keyterm:Strength`); Faithfulness (ID: `keyterm:Faithfulness`); Redeem (ID: `keyterm:Redeem`); Plea for Mercy (ID: `keyterm:Supplication`); To Be Kindly Disposed (ID: `keyterm:KindlyDisposed`); Hell (ID: `place:Hell`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); House (ID: `realia:House`); Net (ID: `realia:Net`); Fortification (ID: `realia:Fortification`); Casting Net (ID: `realia:CastingNet`)

## Psalms 31:2 (id: 24380)

The psalmist urgently asks the Lord to notice his desperate situation ([PSalms 71:2](https://ref.ly/Ps71:2); [88:2](https://ref.ly/Ps88:2); [102:2](https://ref.ly/Ps102:2)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 31:2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** House (ID: `realia:House`); Fortress (ID: `realia:Fortress`)

## Psalms 31:5 (id: 24381)

*Into Your hands I commit my spirit*: The psalmist will trust his Lord, whether he lives or dies. Jesus said these words on the cross before he died ([Luke 23:46](https://ref.ly/Luke23:46)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 31:5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); Redeem (ID: `keyterm:Redeem`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 31:6–8 (id: 24382)

Trusting the Lord means believing he cares about our well\-being.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 31:6-8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 31:8 (id: 24383)

*in the open*: This means a wide area, unlike a narrow or tight place that causes distress (see [Psalm 18:19](https://ref.ly/Ps18:19)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 31:8


## Psalms 31:9–13 (id: 24384)

In this prayer for mercy, the psalmist places a description of his intense suffering and the pain of rejection between two statements of trust in the Lord ([Psalm 31:6](https://ref.ly/Ps31:6), [14](https://ref.ly/Ps31:14)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 31:9-13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Strength (ID: `keyterm:Strength`); To Be Kindly Disposed (ID: `keyterm:KindlyDisposed`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 31:10 (id: 24372)

*my bones are wasting away*: This phrase powerfully describes how despair can harm our physical health.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 31:10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Strength (ID: `keyterm:Strength`)

## Psalms 31:14–18 (id: 24373)

Hope does not trust weak and unreliable human beings. It trusts only in the Lord. The suffering person places his life in God’s hands while he waits for the Lord to bring justice.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 31:14-18

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Hell (ID: `place:Hell`)

## Psalms 31:15 (id: 24374)

*My times are in Your hands*: To give up control of timing is one way to express submission.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 31:15


## Psalms 31:17 (id: 24375)

The psalmist, who did not deserve to be disgraced*,* invokes the principle of retribution (see study note on Psalm 1:6; see also “Prayers for Vengeance” Theme Note).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 31:17
* **Associated Articles:** Psalms 1:6 (ID: `23658@TyndaleStudyNotes`); Prayers for Vengeance (ID: `500274@TyndaleStudyNotesThemes`)

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Hell (ID: `place:Hell`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 31:19–20 (id: 24376)

The psalmist, who did not deserve to be disgraced*,* invokes the principle of retribution 

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 31:19-20

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`)

## Psalms 31:19–24 (id: 24377)

The psalmist thanks the Lord for answering his prayer and rescuing him ([Psalm 31:1–18](https://ref.ly/Ps31:1-Ps31:18)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 31:19-24

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Faithfulness (ID: `keyterm:Faithfulness`); Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`); Loyal Person (ID: `keyterm:LoyalPerson`); Plea for Mercy (ID: `keyterm:Supplication`); Under Siege (ID: `keyterm:StateOfBeingUnderSiege`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Fortification (ID: `realia:Fortification`)

## Psalms 31:21–22 (id: 24378)

*Blessed be the LORD*: The psalmist expresses his joy at the experience of God’s goodness and love. Throughout his struggles, the Lord has cared for him.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 31:21-22

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Plea for Mercy (ID: `keyterm:Supplication`); Under Siege (ID: `keyterm:StateOfBeingUnderSiege`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Fortification (ID: `realia:Fortification`)

## Psalms 31:23–24 (id: 24379)

Out of his own pain and renewed awareness of the Lord’s goodness, the psalmist turns to others who suffer. He holds out the promise of God’s loving care for those who persevere in hoping for the Lord.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 31:23-24

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Loyal Person (ID: `keyterm:LoyalPerson`); Faithfulness (ID: `keyterm:Faithfulness`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`)

## Psalms 32:1–2 (id: 24386)

The Lord’s forgiveness brings people back into a relationship with him, removes their guilt, and gives them joy. Paul quoted this text when explaining justification by faith alone ([Romans 4:4–8](https://ref.ly/Rom4:4-Rom4:8)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 32:1-2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Forgive (ID: `keyterm:Forgive`); David (ID: `person:David`); Deceit (ID: `keyterm:Deceit`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`)

## Psalms 32:1–11 (id: 24385)

This wisdom psalm of confession and thanks urges the godly to learn from the psalmist’s journey through sin, denial, confession, and forgiveness. The early church used this psalm as one of the seven penitential psalms (psalms of repentance, see also [Psalms 6](https://ref.ly/Ps6:1-Ps6:10), [38](https://ref.ly/Ps38:1-Ps38:22), [51](https://ref.ly/Ps51:1-Ps51:19), [102](https://ref.ly/Ps102:1-Ps102:28), [130](https://ref.ly/Ps130:1-Ps130:8), [143](https://ref.ly/Ps143:1-Ps143:12)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 32:1-11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Forgive (ID: `keyterm:Forgive`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); To Sin (ID: `keyterm:Sin`); Have Insight (ID: `keyterm:HaveInsight`); Loyal Person (ID: `keyterm:LoyalPerson`); David (ID: `person:David`); Deceit (ID: `keyterm:Deceit`); Repent (ID: `keyterm:Repent`); Perceive (ID: `keyterm:Perceive`); Bit and Bridle (ID: `realia:BitAndBridle`); Horse (ID: `fauna:Horse`); Mule (ID: `fauna:Mule`)

## Psalms 32:3–5 (id: 24388)

The psalmist admits his sin (see [Psalms 19:12–13](https://ref.ly/Ps19:12-Ps19:13); [25:7](https://ref.ly/Ps25:7), [11](https://ref.ly/Ps25:11), [15–22](https://ref.ly/Ps25:15-Ps25:22)) and his resistance to confessing sin. The Lord pressed him hard until he confessed his guilt.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 32:3-5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** To Sin (ID: `keyterm:Sin`); Forgive (ID: `keyterm:Forgive`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Repent (ID: `keyterm:Repent`)

## Psalms 32:4 (id: 24389)

The psalmist believes his suffering comes directly from the Lord's discipline (compare [Psalms 38:2](https://ref.ly/Ps38:2); [39:10](https://ref.ly/Ps39:10)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 32:4


## Psalms 32:5 (id: 24390)

God has forgiven the guilt and rebellion mentioned in [Psalm 32:1](https://ref.ly/Ps32:1). The Lord’s nature is to reconcile and forgive ([Psalms 78:38](https://ref.ly/Ps78:38); [85:2](https://ref.ly/Ps85:2); [130:4](https://ref.ly/Ps130:4)), but he requires people to confess and repent.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 32:5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** To Sin (ID: `keyterm:Sin`); Forgive (ID: `keyterm:Forgive`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Repent (ID: `keyterm:Repent`)

## Psalms 32:6 (id: 24391)

The godly will learn from the psalmist’s experience to pray for forgiveness before it is too late.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 32:6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Loyal Person (ID: `keyterm:LoyalPerson`)

## Psalms 32:7 (id: 24392)

The Lord promises to be a safe place for his people ([Psalm 31:20](https://ref.ly/Ps31:20)). Instead of feeling terrified ([Psalm 31:13](https://ref.ly/Ps31:13)), the psalmist hears songs of victory all around.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 32:7


## Psalms 32:8 (id: 24393)

God interrupts the psalmist's teaching with a message inviting the godly to receive the Lord's wisdom (see [Psalms 5:8](https://ref.ly/Ps5:8); [23:3](https://ref.ly/Ps23:3); [25:4–6](https://ref.ly/Ps25:4-Ps25:6)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 32:8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Have Insight (ID: `keyterm:HaveInsight`)

## Psalms 32:9 (id: 24394)

The foolish person lacks wisdom. Ignoring the Lord’s advice harms life and makes a person like an animal (see [Isaiah 1:3](https://ref.ly/Isa1:3); [James 3:3](https://ref.ly/Jas3:3)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 32:9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Perceive (ID: `keyterm:Perceive`); Horse (ID: `fauna:Horse`); Bit and Bridle (ID: `realia:BitAndBridle`); Mule (ID: `fauna:Mule`)

## Psalms 32:10 (id: 24387)

Difficulties common to all of life will come to the wicked, whereas the Lord promises to protect the godly with his care.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 32:10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 33:1–3 (id: 24396)

The psalmist urges the godly to praise the Lord with melodies*,* with a new song*,* and with skillfully\-played music.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 33:1-3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Lute (ID: `realia:Lute`); Lyre (ID: `realia:Lyre`)

## Psalms 33:1–22 (id: 24395)

This hymn of creation (see also [Psalms 8](https://ref.ly/Ps8:1-Ps8:9), [19](https://ref.ly/Ps19:1-Ps19:14), [104](https://ref.ly/Ps104:1-Ps104:35), [145](https://ref.ly/Ps145:1-Ps145:21)) might be a continuation of [Psalm 32](https://ref.ly/Ps32:1-Ps32:11). It urges readers to praise God ([Psalm 33:1–3](https://ref.ly/Ps33:1-Ps33:3)), to recognize the power of his word in creation ([33:4–7](https://ref.ly/Ps33:4-Ps33:7)), and to respect the Creator ([33:8–11](https://ref.ly/Ps33:8-Ps33:11)). It gives hope to forgiven sinners ([Psalm 32](https://ref.ly/Ps32:1-Ps32:11)) that they can live a new life in the fear of the Lord and under divine protection.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 33:1-22

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Counsel (ID: `keyterm:Counsel`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Word of the Lord (ID: `keyterm:WordOfTheLord`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Strength (ID: `keyterm:Strength`); Perceive (ID: `keyterm:Perceive`); Shield (ID: `keyterm:Shield`); To Cause to Possess (ID: `keyterm:Possess`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Lute (ID: `realia:Lute`); Lyre (ID: `realia:Lyre`); Horse (ID: `fauna:Horse`); Small Shield (ID: `realia:SmallShield`)

## Psalms 33:4–7 (id: 24405)

God's word shows his character and power.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 33:4-7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Word of the Lord (ID: `keyterm:WordOfTheLord`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`)

## Psalms 33:7 (id: 24406)

The nations surrounding Israel thought that the sea had divine power, but it is under God’s control, within boundaries that he set (see [Psalm 104:8–10](https://ref.ly/Ps104:8-Ps104:10)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 33:7


## Psalms 33:8 (id: 24408)

The power of the Lord extends to all the nations (see Psalm 22; [96:123](https://ref.ly/Ps96:1-Ps96:3)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 33:8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 33:8–11 (id: 24407)

Fear of the Lord begins with awe for God and for his powerful word. His word created everything and continues to order and sustain creation. He frustrates the plans of people who oppose his will.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 33:8-11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Counsel (ID: `keyterm:Counsel`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`)

## Psalms 33:10 (id: 24397)

The plans of powerful people will only succeed if God allows it (see [Isaiah 8:9210](https://ref.ly/Isa8:9-Isa8:10)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 33:10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Counsel (ID: `keyterm:Counsel`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 33:11 (id: 24398)

* God’s word still orders creation and will do so forever.
* God's purposes are wonderful ([Psalms 40:5](https://ref.ly/Ps40:5); [92:5](https://ref.ly/Ps92:5)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 33:11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Counsel (ID: `keyterm:Counsel`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 33:12 (id: 24399)

People who fear God are chosen as his inheritance (see [Psalm 33:13–19](https://ref.ly/Ps33:13-Ps33:19)). This does not include rebellious people ([Psalm 78:62](https://ref.ly/Ps78:62)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 33:12

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** To Cause to Possess (ID: `keyterm:Possess`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 33:13–19 (id: 24400)

From the heavens, the Lord searches out and cares for anyone who fears him (see [Psalm 14:2–3](https://ref.ly/Ps14:2-Ps14:3)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 33:13-19

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Strength (ID: `keyterm:Strength`); Perceive (ID: `keyterm:Perceive`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Horse (ID: `fauna:Horse`)

## Psalms 33:16–17 (id: 24401)

*army …great strength. … horse*: Even military power is under God's control (see [Psalm 33:10–11](https://ref.ly/Ps33:10-Ps33:11); [Ecclesiastes 9:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:11)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 33:16-17

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Strength (ID: `keyterm:Strength`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Horse (ID: `fauna:Horse`)

## Psalms 33:18 (id: 24402)

God watches over all who live by his counsel ([Psalm 32:8](https://ref.ly/Ps32:8)). He protects, rescues, and stays close to them ([Psalm 34:15–22](https://ref.ly/Ps34:15-Ps34:22)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 33:18

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 33:20–22 (id: 24403)

The faithful see God's power in creation and world events. They respond with trust, commitment, joy, and hope as they pray and wait for God's help.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 33:20-22

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Shield (ID: `keyterm:Shield`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Small Shield (ID: `realia:SmallShield`)

## Psalms 33:22 (id: 24404)

The psalmist uses wordplay to contrast his people's certain hope (Hebrew *yakhal*) in the Lord with the supposed strength (Hebrew *khayil*) of the wicked's horses.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 33:22

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 34:1 (id: 24419)

Abimelech is another name for Achish (see [1 Samuel 21:10–15](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:10-1Sam21:15)). The body of the psalm makes no explicit connection to this event.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 34:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 34:1–3 (id: 24410)

The psalmist encourages those in need to join him in praise.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 34:1-3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Poor (ID: `keyterm:Poor`)

## Psalms 34:1–22 (id: 24409)

This wisdom psalm includes a thanksgiving song of praise ([Psalm 34:1–7](https://ref.ly/Ps34:1-Ps34:7)) that celebrates the Lord for caring for and protecting godly sufferers. It also includes an invitation to wisdom ([Psalm 34:8–14](https://ref.ly/Ps34:8-Ps34:14)) and explains wisdom concerning the Lord’s care for the needy and the suffering of the wicked ([Psalm 34:15–22](https://ref.ly/Ps34:15-Ps34:22)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 34:1-22

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); An Angel (ID: `deity:Angel`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`); Deceit (ID: `keyterm:Deceit`); Redeem (ID: `keyterm:Redeem`); Poor (ID: `keyterm:Poor`); Lion (ID: `fauna:Lion`)

## Psalms 34:5–6 (id: 24421)

The psalmist describes his experiences of answered prayer.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 34:5-6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 34:7 (id: 24422)

The *angel of the LORD* showed God's presence during the journey through the wilderness. He protected Israel from Egypt's forces ([Exodus 14:19–20](https://ref.ly/Exod14:19-Exod14:20)), and he will guard all those who are godly ([Psalm 91:11](https://ref.ly/Ps91:11)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 34:7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); An Angel (ID: `deity:Angel`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 34:8–14 (id: 24423)

The godly encourage everyone to seek wisdom, to fear the Lord, and to place themselves under the protection of the Lord’s angel.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 34:8-14

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`); Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); Deceit (ID: `keyterm:Deceit`); Lion (ID: `fauna:Lion`)

## Psalms 34:9–10 (id: 24424)

Fear of the Lord includes reverence and respect, and it motivates a life of wisdom (see [Proverbs 1:7](https://ref.ly/Prov1:7); [9:10](https://ref.ly/Prov9:10)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 34:9-10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`); Lion (ID: `fauna:Lion`)

## Psalms 34:11 (id: 24412)

Anyone who listens, even children*,* can become wise followers of God.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 34:11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 34:11–13 (id: 24411)

Wisdom has three parts: 

1. fearing the Lord ([Psalm 34:11](https://ref.ly/Ps34:11)),
2. doing good ([Psalm 34:14](https://ref.ly/Ps34:14)), and
3. rejecting evil ([Psalm 34:13](https://ref.ly/Ps34:13)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 34:11-13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Deceit (ID: `keyterm:Deceit`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 34:12–16 (id: 24413)

Peter quotes this passage in his instructions for peaceful living ([1 Peter 3:10–12](https://ref.ly/1Pet3:10-1Pet3:12)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 34:12-16

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Deceit (ID: `keyterm:Deceit`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`)

## Psalms 34:13 (id: 24414)

Godly people choose their words carefully. Speaking evil and telling lies is unwise (see [Psalm 10:7](https://ref.ly/Ps10:7); compare [17:1](https://ref.ly/Ps17:1)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 34:13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Deceit (ID: `keyterm:Deceit`)

## Psalms 34:14 (id: 24415)

The way of peace leads to harmony and order, whereas evil destroys them (see [Psalm 37:11](https://ref.ly/Ps37:11)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 34:14

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`)

## Psalms 34:15–16 (id: 24416)

The Lord distinguishes between wise and foolish people; he shows his concern for the wise by rescuing them from trouble ([Psalm 34:15](https://ref.ly/Ps34:15), [17–20](https://ref.ly/Ps34:17-Ps34:20)). However, God destroys the foolish ([Psalm 34:16](https://ref.ly/Ps34:16), [21](https://ref.ly/Ps34:21)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 34:15-16

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 34:18 (id: 24417)

Those who are brokenhearted have a spirit of deep sorrow for sin and a strong dependence on the Lord. God accepts this humble spirit as a proper sacrifice ([Psalm 51:17](https://ref.ly/Ps51:17)). The wicked hate people who are brokenhearted ([Psalm 109:16](https://ref.ly/Ps109:16)). But the Lord heals them and restores them ([Psalm 147:3](https://ref.ly/Ps147:3); see [Isaiah 57:15](https://ref.ly/Isa57:15); [61:1](https://ref.ly/Isa61:1)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 34:18

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 34:19–22 (id: 24418)

The godly might suffer, but the Lord will reward them in the end. Similarly, the wicked might prosper for a time, but ultimately they will perish ([Psalms 1:6](https://ref.ly/Ps1:6); [34:15–16](https://ref.ly/Ps34:15-Ps34:16)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 34:19-22

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`); Redeem (ID: `keyterm:Redeem`)

## Psalms 34:20 (id: 24420)

* Bones represent a person's whole self (see [Psalm 6:2](https://ref.ly/Ps6:2)).
* *not one of them will be broken*: John applied this statement to Jesus at the crucifixion ([John 19:36](https://ref.ly/John19:36)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 34:20


## Psalms 35:1–28 (id: 24425)

In this lament, the Lord's troubled servant asks God to notice his situation and defend him.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 35:1-28

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); An Angel (ID: `deity:Angel`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Casting Net (ID: `realia:CastingNet`); Net (ID: `realia:Net`); David (ID: `person:David`); Deceit (ID: `keyterm:Deceit`); Small Shield (ID: `realia:SmallShield`); Battle-Axe (ID: `realia:Battle-axe`); Sackcloth (ID: `realia:Sackcloth`); Large Shield (ID: `realia:LargeShield`); Lion (ID: `fauna:Lion`); Spear (ID: `realia:Spear`)

## Psalms 35:4–10 (id: 24434)

The psalmist desires the Lord's salvation. The language may seem harsh, but it comes from a wish for justice and for the Lord to help the needy and oppressed.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 35:4-10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); An Angel (ID: `deity:Angel`); Casting Net (ID: `realia:CastingNet`); Net (ID: `realia:Net`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`)

## Psalms 35:6 (id: 24435)

* A *dark and slick* path suggests instability and sudden disaster under the Lord's judgment (see [Psalms 69:23](https://ref.ly/Ps69:23); [73:18](https://ref.ly/Ps73:18); [82:5](https://ref.ly/Ps82:5); [107:10](https://ref.ly/Ps107:10); [143:3](https://ref.ly/Ps143:3)).
* The *angel of the LORD* is an agent of judgment as well as salvation.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 35:6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** An Angel (ID: `deity:Angel`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 35:10 (id: 24426)

Nothing in the universe can compare with the Lord (see [Psalms 86:8](https://ref.ly/Ps86:8); [89:6](https://ref.ly/Ps89:6), [8](https://ref.ly/Ps89:8); [Isaiah 40:18](https://ref.ly/Isa40:18), [25](https://ref.ly/Isa40:25)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 35:10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 35:11 (id: 24428)

False witnesses want nothing but violence (see [Psalms 11:4–6](https://ref.ly/Ps11:4-Ps11:6); [54:3](https://ref.ly/Ps54:3)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 35:11


## Psalms 35:11–18 (id: 24427)

The psalmist presents the Lord with the reasons for his suffering. Evil has many faces. He is tired and worn out, and he knows that only the Lord can help him.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 35:11-18

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Lion (ID: `fauna:Lion`); Sackcloth (ID: `realia:Sackcloth`)

## Psalms 35:16 (id: 24429)

The psalmist's attackers *gnashed their teeth* to show their jealousy.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 35:16


## Psalms 35:19 (id: 24431)

John applied this claim of innocence (see also [Psalm 69:4](https://ref.ly/Ps69:4)) to Jesus ([John 15:25](https://ref.ly/John15:25)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 35:19


## Psalms 35:19–25 (id: 24430)

In this prayer for justice, the psalmist faces a crisis so great that only the Lord can resolve it. The psalmist chooses the right path and believes that vengeance belongs to the Lord.

God’s patience allows the wicked to continue in their evil ways. Because of this, their hearts grow harder and they find reasons to justify their behavior.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 35:19-25

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Deceit (ID: `keyterm:Deceit`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`)

## Psalms 35:21 (id: 24432)

*Aha, aha!*: This Hebrew word (*he’akh*) expresses a feeling of joy.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 35:21


## Psalms 35:26–28 (id: 24433)

The psalmist calls on the Lord to judge the wicked and to rescue the godly and bring them joy.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 35:26-28

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 36:1 (id: 24440)

*no fear of God*: Paul uses this phrase to describe how all people are morally corrupt ([Romans 3:18](https://ref.ly/Rom3:18)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 36:1

## Psalms 36:1–4 (id: 24437)

Foolish people reject wisdom and goodness while they chase after evil.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 36:1-4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); Have Insight (ID: `keyterm:HaveInsight`); Deceit (ID: `keyterm:Deceit`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Bed (ID: `realia:Bed`)

## Psalms 36:1–12 (id: 24436)

This lament contrasts the foolish world made by the wicked with the wise world created and maintained by the Lord. The wicked care only about themselves, while the Lord cares for all creation. He will step in to bring justice to his world. The righteous will enjoy his protection, but the wicked will die.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 36:1-12

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Have Insight (ID: `keyterm:HaveInsight`); Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`); Deceit (ID: `keyterm:Deceit`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); House (ID: `realia:House`); Bed (ID: `realia:Bed`); Cattle (ID: `fauna:Bull`)

## Psalms 36:5–6 (id: 24441)

God’s all\-encompassing goodness surpasses that of the created order. God graciously meets his people’s practical needs.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 36:5-6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Cattle (ID: `fauna:Bull`)

## Psalms 37:1 (id: 24454)

*envy*: The jealous desire to have what others enjoy leads to further sin ([Psalm 73:3](https://ref.ly/Ps73:3); [Proverbs 3:31](https://ref.ly/Prov3:31); [James 1:14–15](https://ref.ly/Jas1:14-Jas1:15); [4:1–2](https://ref.ly/Jas4:1-Jas4:2)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 37:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** David (ID: `person:David`); Be Zealous (ID: `keyterm:Zealous`)

## Psalms 37:1–9 (id: 24447)

The wise respond to evil by trusting in the Lord. Trust has five parts:

1. Do not become irritated or jealous of those who do evil ([Psalm 37:1–2](https://ref.ly/Ps37:1-Ps37:2)).
2. Delight in the Lord ([Psalm 37:3–4](https://ref.ly/Ps37:3-Ps37:4)).
3. Submit to the Lord ([Psalm 37:5–6](https://ref.ly/Ps37:5-Ps37:6)).
4. Practice patience in hope ([Psalm 37:7](https://ref.ly/Ps37:7)).
5. Avoid anger ([Psalm 37:8–9](https://ref.ly/Ps37:8-Ps37:9)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 37:1-9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Plan (ID: `keyterm:Plan`); Be Zealous (ID: `keyterm:Zealous`); David (ID: `person:David`); Dispossess (ID: `keyterm:Dispossess`); Green Vegetation (ID: `flora:GreenVegetation`); Grass (ID: `flora:Grass`)

## Psalms 37:1–40 (id: 24446)

This psalm addresses the issues raised in [Psalm 36](https://ref.ly/Ps36:1-Ps36:12): 

* How should the godly react to evil?
* When will the Lord bring justice?
* Why does he allow the wicked to harm his creation?

[Psalm 37](https://ref.ly/Ps37:1-Ps37:40) provides clear answers that reduce the tensions of life ([Psalm 37:1–9](https://ref.ly/Ps37:1-Ps37:9), [34](https://ref.ly/Ps37:34)) and promise that the meek will have a future ([Psalm 37:10–11](https://ref.ly/Ps37:10-Ps37:11), [39–40](https://ref.ly/Ps37:39-Ps37:40)). 

The contrast between the godly and the wicked highlights the uselessness of foolishness ([Psalm 37:12–24](https://ref.ly/Ps37:12-Ps37:24), [27–33](https://ref.ly/Ps37:27-Ps37:33)). The psalmist's personal insights add a human element ([Psalm 37:25–26](https://ref.ly/Ps37:25-Ps37:26), [35–38](https://ref.ly/Ps37:35-Ps37:38)). He encourages viewing the land's inheritance with a long\-term perspective: The earth belongs to the Lord, who will give it to those he blesses—the humble and godly who trust, hope, and follow him (see [Psalm 37:9](https://ref.ly/Ps37:9), [11](https://ref.ly/Ps37:11), [22](https://ref.ly/Ps37:22), [29](https://ref.ly/Ps37:29), [34](https://ref.ly/Ps37:34); see also [Isaiah 57:13](https://ref.ly/Isa57:13); [Matthew 5:5](https://ref.ly/Matt5:5)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 37:1-40

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Dispossess (ID: `keyterm:Dispossess`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); To Be Kindly Disposed (ID: `keyterm:KindlyDisposed`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Loyal Person (ID: `keyterm:LoyalPerson`); Mighty (ID: `keyterm:Mighty.2`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Bow (ID: `realia:Bow`); Sword (ID: `realia:Sword`); Instruction (ID: `keyterm:Instruction`); David (ID: `person:David`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Poor (ID: `keyterm:Poor`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); Be Zealous (ID: `keyterm:Zealous`); The Way (ID: `keyterm:TheWay`); To Declare Guilty (ID: `keyterm:DeclareGuilty`); Plan (ID: `keyterm:Plan`); Complete (ID: `keyterm:Complete`); Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`); To Cause to Possess (ID: `keyterm:Possess`); Ability (ID: `keyterm:Ability`); Perceive (ID: `keyterm:Perceive`); Cedar (ID: `flora:Cedar`); Stone Pine (ID: `flora:Pine`); Grass (ID: `flora:Grass`); Fortress (ID: `realia:Fortress`); Green Vegetation (ID: `flora:GreenVegetation`)

## Psalms 38:3 (id: 24481)

Sin can lead to sickness and even death ([1 Corinthians 11:30](https://ref.ly/1Cor11:30)). The psalmist may have felt physically ill, or his sickness was a metaphor for emotional distress. ## Psalms 38:22 (id: 24480)

In the Psalms, people often ask the Lord to act quickly in times of great need ([Psalms 22:19](https://ref.ly/Ps22:19); [31:2](https://ref.ly/Ps31:2); [40:13](https://ref.ly/Ps40:13); [69:17](https://ref.ly/Ps69:17); [70:1](https://ref.ly/Ps70:1); [71:12](https://ref.ly/Ps71:12); [79:8](https://ref.ly/Ps79:8); [102:2](https://ref.ly/Ps102:2); [141:1](https://ref.ly/Ps141:1); [143:7](https://ref.ly/Ps143:7)). However, wisdom and experience show that God's people often need to wait for him to act ([Psalms 27:14](https://ref.ly/Ps27:14); [37:7](https://ref.ly/Ps37:7); [Isaiah 8:17](https://ref.ly/Isa8:17); [40:31](https://ref.ly/Isa40:31)).

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Handbreadth (ID: `realia:Handbreadth`)

## Psalms 39:4–6 (id: 24492)

Due to his suffering and sin, the psalmist concludes that life is meaningless and short (see [Job 7:7–10](https://ref.ly/Job7:7-Job7:10); [Isaiah 40:6–8](https://ref.ly/Isa40:6-Isa40:8)). He had expected only a wicked life to be like that (see [Psalms 37:2](https://ref.ly/Ps37:2), [20](https://ref.ly/Ps37:20); [103:15–16](https://ref.ly/Ps103:15-Ps103:16)), but now it seems irrelevant whether a person is godly or not.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 39:4-6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Likeness (ID: `keyterm:Semblance`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Handbreadth (ID: `realia:Handbreadth`)

## Psalms 39:8 (id: 24493)

The idea that rebellion leads to suffering is a common theme in [Psalms 32](https://ref.ly/Ps32:1-Ps32:11), [38–41](https://ref.ly/Ps38:1-Ps41:13).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 39:8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`)

## Psalms 39:9–11 (id: 24494)

The psalmist links suffering with God’s discipline, assuming that it is punishment for his sin.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 39:9-11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Moth (ID: `fauna:Moth`)

## Psalms 39:11 (id: 24487)

The Lord uses discipline to correct his children ([Psalms 38:1](https://ref.ly/Ps38:1), [3](https://ref.ly/Ps38:3), [7](https://ref.ly/Ps38:7); [40:12](https://ref.ly/Ps40:12); see [Proverbs 3:11–12](https://ref.ly/Prov3:11-Prov3:12); [Hebrews 12:5–6](https://ref.ly/Heb12:5-Heb12:6)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 39:11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Moth (ID: `fauna:Moth`)

## Psalms 39:12 (id: 24488)

A *foreigner* could not own land (see [Leviticus 25:23](https://ref.ly/Lev25:23)). The patriarchs had lived as guests in Canaan ([Psalm 105:23](https://ref.ly/Ps105:23)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 39:12

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 39:13 (id: 24489)

Earlier, the psalmist asked God to stay close to him ([Psalm 8:21–22](https://ref.ly/Ps38:21-Ps38:22)). Now, he wants God to leave him alone. This is similar to how Job felt ([Job 7:17–19](https://ref.ly/Job7:17-Job7:19)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 39:13


## Psalms 40:1–5 (id: 24503)

The psalmist thanks the Lord for rescue and affirms that people can trust God.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 40:1-5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Safety (ID: `keyterm:Safety`); Pit (ID: `realia:Pit`)

## Psalms 40:1–17 (id: 24502)

This psalm has a thanksgiving song ([Psalm 40:1–10](https://ref.ly/Ps40:1-Ps40:10)) and a lament ([40:11–17](https://ref.ly/Ps40:11-Ps40:17)). The thanksgiving song explains why the psalmist is thankful ([Psalm 40:1–5](https://ref.ly/Ps40:1-Ps40:5)), shows a promise to stay committed ([40:6–8](https://ref.ly/Ps40:6-Ps40:8)), and shares a public testimony about God's character ([40:7–10](https://ref.ly/Ps40:7-Ps40:10)). The lament talks about the psalmist's troubles due to his sins ([Psalm 40:11–12](https://ref.ly/Ps40:11-Ps40:12)) and includes two prayers asking for justice ([40:13–15](https://ref.ly/Ps40:13-Ps40:15), [17](https://ref.ly/Ps40:17)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 40:1-17

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Delight (ID: `keyterm:Delight`); Slaughtering (ID: `keyterm:Slaughtering`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Safety (ID: `keyterm:Safety`); Instruction (ID: `keyterm:Instruction`); Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); A Group of People (ID: `group:GenericGroup`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Pit (ID: `realia:Pit`); Scroll (ID: `realia:Scroll`)

## Psalms 40:6 (id: 24506)

* The Lord delights in the attitude and character of the person giving an offering more than the offering itself.
* *my ears You have opened*: The Greek text reads *You have given me a body.* In [Hebrews 10:5–7](https://ref.ly/Heb10:5-Heb10:7), the writer quotes from the Greek version of [Psalm 40:6–8](https://ref.ly/Ps40:6-Ps40:8). He presents these words as something Jesus said when he left heaven and came into the world.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 40:6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); Slaughtering (ID: `keyterm:Slaughtering`)

## Psalms 40:7 (id: 24507)

*it is written about me in the scroll*: Moses had recorded God’s requirements for Israel’s kings in [Deuteronomy 17:14–15](https://ref.ly/Deut17:14-Deut17:15).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 40:7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Scroll (ID: `realia:Scroll`)

## Psalms 41:1 (id: 24511)

The poor who suffer hardship have special protection under God's law ([Leviticus 14:21](https://ref.ly/Lev14:21); [19:10](https://ref.ly/Lev19:10)). They receive God's justice and guidance ([Psalms 72:13](https://ref.ly/Ps72:13); [82:3–4](https://ref.ly/Ps82:3-Ps82:4)). The word translated as *poor* often means the godly ([Psalm 113:7](https://ref.ly/Ps113:7); see [Zephaniah 3:12](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:12)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 41:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Have Insight (ID: `keyterm:HaveInsight`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 41:1–13 (id: 24508)

In this wisdom psalm, the psalmist: 

* asks for healing ([Psalm 41:4](https://ref.ly/Ps41:4), [10](https://ref.ly/Ps41:10)),
* laments his distress ([Psalm 41:5–9](https://ref.ly/Ps41:5-Ps41:9)), and
* expresses confidence in the Lord's blessing ([Psalm 41:1–3](https://ref.ly/Ps41:1-Ps41:3), [11–12](https://ref.ly/Ps41:11-Ps41:12)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 41:1-13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); To Be Kindly Disposed (ID: `keyterm:KindlyDisposed`); Wickedness (ID: `keyterm:Wickedness`); Surely! (ID: `keyterm:Surely`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); To Sin (ID: `keyterm:Sin`); Have Insight (ID: `keyterm:HaveInsight`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); A Group of People (ID: `group:GenericGroup`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Bed (ID: `realia:Bed`)

## Psalms 41:3 (id: 24512)

*on his bed of illness*: This illness might be physical or spiritual (see [Psalm 6:2](https://ref.ly/Ps6:2); [Psalm 38](https://ref.ly/Ps38:1-Ps38:22)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 41:3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Bed (ID: `realia:Bed`)

## Psalms 41:4 (id: 24513)

The psalmist applies the blessing to himself ([Psalm 41:12](https://ref.ly/Ps41:12)). He has helped the poor ([Psalm 41:1](https://ref.ly/Ps41:1)), but he has sinned against the Lord in other ways. In his pain, he asks for healing and admits his sin. Only the Lord can provide the restoration that brings true joy ([Psalm 30:2](https://ref.ly/Ps30:2); [107:20–21](https://ref.ly/Ps107:20-Ps107:21)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 41:4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** To Sin (ID: `keyterm:Sin`); To Be Kindly Disposed (ID: `keyterm:KindlyDisposed`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 41:5 (id: 24515)

*When will he die*: The psalmist's enemies see his suffering as a judgment from God (see [Psalm 1:6](https://ref.ly/Ps1:6)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 41:5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** A Group of People (ID: `group:GenericGroup`)

## Psalms 41:5–9 (id: 24514)

The psalmist’s enemies disguise themselves as friends, while his friends openly become his enemies.

## Psalms 41:9 (id: 24516)

*the one who shared my bread*: Eating together was a sign of unity ([Acts 2:42](https://ref.ly/Acts2:42)). Refusing to eat with someone was a sign of hostility ([1 Corinthians 5:11](https://ref.ly/1Cor5:11)). Jesus experienced the same kind of betrayal ([John 13:1](https://ref.ly/John13:1), [18](https://ref.ly/John13:18)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 41:9

This doxology (song of praise) ends Book One ([Psalms 1–41](https://ref.ly/Ps1:1-Ps41:13)). Compare with [Psalm 106:48](https://ref.ly/Ps106:48).

## Psalms 42:1 (id: 24519)

The sons of Korah were members of a Levitical choir. They were chosen by David to serve as singers in the temple (see [1 Chronicles 6:22–38](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:22-1Chr6:38); [9:19–34](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:19-1Chr9:34); [2 Chronicles 20:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:19)). Their ancestor Korah was the rebel who opposed Moses and was swallowed by the earth ([Exodus 6:24](https://ref.ly/Exod6:24); [Numbers 16](https://ref.ly/Num16:1-Num16:50)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 42:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Deer (ID: `fauna:Deer`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 42:1–4 (id: 24518)

* The psalmist's deep desire for God arises from being far away from the temple as he remembers the past.
* The verb translated as "longs" appears only here and in [Joel 1:20](https://ref.ly/Joel1:20) (meaning "cry out"). In both instances, it describes extreme thirst in a dry desert (see [Psalms 63:1](https://ref.ly/Ps63:1); [143:6](https://ref.ly/Ps143:6); see also [84:2](https://ref.ly/Ps84:2)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 42:1-4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Praise (ID: `keyterm:Praise`); Deer (ID: `fauna:Deer`); House (ID: `realia:House`)

## Psalms 42:1–43.5 (id: 24517)

These psalms, like [Psalms 9](https://ref.ly/Ps9:1-Ps9:20) and [10](https://ref.ly/Ps10:1-Ps10:18), form a unit with a lament and a bittersweet refrain of hope ([Psalm 42:5](https://ref.ly/Ps42:5), [11](https://ref.ly/Ps42:11); [43:5](https://ref.ly/Ps43:5)). Many Hebrew manuscripts combine these two psalms into one. 

The historical context is unclear; these psalms might represent God's people in exile, expressing loyalty to God while he punishes them for their ongoing disobedience. 

The psalmist longs for the fellowship with God he had in Jerusalem ([Psalm 42:4](https://ref.ly/Ps42:4)); his memories only deepen his discouragement. As the psalmist cries out to God, he remembers God's love and faithfulness ([Psalm 42:8](https://ref.ly/Ps42:8)), which leads him to ask for vindication ([43:1–4](https://ref.ly/Ps43:1-Ps43:4)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 42:1-43:5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Mount Mizar (ID: `place:MizarMount`); Loyal Person (ID: `keyterm:LoyalPerson`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Jordan River (ID: `place:Jordan`); Deceit (ID: `keyterm:Deceit`); Praise (ID: `keyterm:Praise`); A Group of People (ID: `group:GenericGroup`); Mount Hermon (ID: `place:HermonMount`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); Deer (ID: `fauna:Deer`); House (ID: `realia:House`); Tabernacle Altar (ID: `realia:TabernacleAltar`); Stone Altar (ID: `realia:StoneAltar`); Fortress (ID: `realia:Fortress`); Altar (ID: `realia:Altar`); Temple Altar (ID: `realia:TempleAltar`); Lute (ID: `realia:Lute`)

* Crying so much that tears become food shows deep sadness ([Psalms 80:5](https://ref.ly/Ps80:5); [102:9](https://ref.ly/Ps102:9); see [6:6](https://ref.ly/Ps6:6); [56:8](https://ref.ly/Ps56:8); [119:136](https://ref.ly/Ps119:136); [126:5](https://ref.ly/Ps126:5)).
* The wicked try to impose their timing on God and deny his power to intervene.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 42:3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 42:4 (id: 24521)

The psalmist remembers leading a large procession as part of the Levitical choir, possibly during the important holy days.

The psalmist feels sad, but he can still function. He remembers that the living God is his Savior. This verse appears again in [Psalms 42:11](https://ref.ly/Ps42:11) and [43:5](https://ref.ly/Ps43:5).

## Psalms 42:6 (id: 24524)

* Mount Hermon is a high mountain range northeast of Israel. The source of the Jordan is by Dan at the foot of Mount Hermon.
* Mount Mizar might be a peak in the Mount Hermon range.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 42:6

## Psalms 43:3 (id: 24530)

* This prayer for God's redemption sees light and truth as guides (see [Psalms 18:25–29](https://ref.ly/Ps18:25-Ps18:29); [27:1](https://ref.ly/Ps27:1); [85:10–13](https://ref.ly/Ps85:10-Ps85:13); [89:14–18](https://ref.ly/Ps89:14-Ps89:18); see also [119:105](https://ref.ly/Ps119:105); [John 3:19](https://ref.ly/John3:19); [Ephesians 5:8](https://ref.ly/Eph5:8); [1 Thessalonians 5:5–7](https://ref.ly/1Thess5:5-1Thess5:7)).
* The temple on the mountain in Jerusalem symbolized God's holy presence among his people ([Psalms 15:1](https://ref.ly/Ps15:1); [43:3](https://ref.ly/Ps43:3); [99:5](https://ref.ly/Ps99:5), [9](https://ref.ly/Ps99:9); [132:7](https://ref.ly/Ps132:7)).

## Psalms 44:1 (id: 24534)

*We have heard … the work You did*: The story of redemption includes God's favor and power in his past acts of rescue (see [Psalm 78:1–4](https://ref.ly/Ps78:1-Ps78:4); see also [Psalms 105–106](https://ref.ly/Ps105:1-Ps106:48)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 44:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 44:1–26 (id: 24532)

This national lament after a defeat in battle continues the tone of the previous two psalms. It reflects on an unspecified moment in Israel's history and calls on God for salvation. The people: 

* recite God's past acts of rescue ([Psalm 44:1–3](https://ref.ly/Ps44:1-Ps44:3)),
* recognize God's power to save ([Psalm 44:4–8](https://ref.ly/Ps44:4-Ps44:8)),
* describe their humiliation in exile ([Psalm 44:9–16](https://ref.ly/Ps44:9-Ps44:16)),
* claim their innocence and lament the injustice of their current situation ([Psalm 44:17–22](https://ref.ly/Ps44:17-Ps44:22)), and
* cry for vindication ([Psalm 44:23–26](https://ref.ly/Ps44:23-Ps44:26)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 44:1-26

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Dispossess (ID: `keyterm:Dispossess`); Sword (ID: `realia:Sword`); Flock (ID: `fauna:Flock`); Goat (ID: `fauna:Goat`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Covenant (ID: `keyterm:Covenant`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Redeem (ID: `keyterm:Redeem`); Proverb (ID: `keyterm:Saying`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Jackal (ID: `fauna:Jackal`); Bow (ID: `realia:Bow`)

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 44:23-26
