## Psalms 45:1 (id: 24545)

*To the tune of "The Lilies"* is no longer known (see also [Psalm 69](https://ref.ly/Ps69:1-Ps69:36)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 45:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Pen (ID: `realia:Pen`)

## Psalms 45:1–17 (id: 24542)

This psalm celebrates a royal wedding; it might have been sung on many occasions and not just at one wedding. As God’s representative, the king carried the responsibility of dispensing justice and maintaining order in God’s world (see [Psalm 2](https://ref.ly/Ps2:1-Ps2:12)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 45:1-17

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Smear (ID: `keyterm:Smear`); Majesty (ID: `keyterm:Majesty`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Tyre (ID: `place:Tyre`); Arrow (ID: `keyterm:Arrow`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); Ophir (ID: `place:Ophir`); Fairness (ID: `keyterm:Fairness`); Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); Favor (ID: `keyterm:Favor`); Ask for (ID: `keyterm:AskFor`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); Pen (ID: `realia:Pen`); Cassia (ID: `flora:Cassia`); Agarwood (ID: `flora:Agarwood`); Aloes (ID: `flora:Aloes`); Anointing Oil (ID: `realia:AnointingOil`); Sword (ID: `realia:Sword`); Embroidered Cloth (ID: `realia:EmbroideredCloth`); Myrrh (ID: `flora:Myrrh`); Arrow (ID: `realia:Arrow`); Olive Oil (ID: `realia:OliveOil`)

## Psalms 45:2 (id: 24546)

This general description of the king’s symbolic status anticipates his enduring legacy ([Psalm 45:16](https://ref.ly/Ps45:16)). The Lord’s blessing and the charge to reflect God’s attributes are symbolized by his handsome appearance and gracious speech.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 45:2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Favor (ID: `keyterm:Favor`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 45:3 (id: 24548)

Glory and majesty belong to the Lord ([Psalms 96:6](https://ref.ly/Ps96:6); [111:3](https://ref.ly/Ps111:3)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 45:3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Majesty (ID: `keyterm:Majesty`); Sword (ID: `realia:Sword`)

## Psalms 45:3–5 (id: 24547)

The psalmist describes the royal splendor the king showed when fighting his enemies.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 45:3-5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Majesty (ID: `keyterm:Majesty`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); Arrow (ID: `keyterm:Arrow`); Sword (ID: `realia:Sword`); Arrow (ID: `realia:Arrow`)

## Psalms 45:4 (id: 24549)

As God's representative of truth, humility, and justice (see [Psalm 72:1–2](https://ref.ly/Ps72:1-Ps72:2), [4](https://ref.ly/Ps72:4), [7](https://ref.ly/Ps72:7)), the king can perform impressive deeds ([65:5](https://ref.ly/Ps65:5); [96:4](https://ref.ly/Ps96:4)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 45:4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`)

## Psalms 45:6 (id: 24551)

Justice characterizes God’s rule*,* as it should characterize the king’s administration.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 45:6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Fairness (ID: `keyterm:Fairness`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 45:6–9 (id: 24550)

* The king is like a god among humans. God anointed him, gave him a powerful throne, and he delivers justice as he rules eternally.
* The writer of Hebrews uses [Psalm 45:6–7](https://ref.ly/Ps45:6-Ps45:7) to describe Jesus, arguing that the Son is greater than the angels ([Hebrews 1:8–9](https://ref.ly/Heb1:8-Heb1:9)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 45:6-9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Smear (ID: `keyterm:Smear`); Ophir (ID: `place:Ophir`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); Fairness (ID: `keyterm:Fairness`); Cassia (ID: `flora:Cassia`); Agarwood (ID: `flora:Agarwood`); Aloes (ID: `flora:Aloes`); Anointing Oil (ID: `realia:AnointingOil`); Myrrh (ID: `flora:Myrrh`); Olive Oil (ID: `realia:OliveOil`)

## Psalms 45:8 (id: 24552)

* Fine spices mixed with oil create unique perfumes.
* The king's palaces had wood panels with ivory inlays (see [Psalm 6:4](https://ref.ly/Ps6:4); [Amos 3:15](https://ref.ly/Amos3:15)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 45:8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Agarwood (ID: `flora:Agarwood`); Cassia (ID: `flora:Cassia`); Aloes (ID: `flora:Aloes`); Myrrh (ID: `flora:Myrrh`)

## Psalms 45:9 (id: 24553)

Royal marriages often confirmed diplomatic alliances between kings, and the women became part of the royal harem (see [1 Kings 11:1–3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:1-1Kgs11:3)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 45:9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Ophir (ID: `place:Ophir`)

## Psalms 45:10–12 (id: 24543)

The bride is a representative of the king. Powerful people and nations try to gain her favor in order to get close to the king. This is a great honor, and she is encouraged to give herself fully to her new position.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 45:10-12

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); Ask for (ID: `keyterm:AskFor`); Tyre (ID: `place:Tyre`)

## Psalms 45:12 (id: 24544)

*The Daughter of Tyr**e*: Israel traded with Tyre, a powerful city\-state. The city's most noble woman brings a wedding gift to the new queen, who represents an honored kingdom.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 45:12

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); Ask for (ID: `keyterm:AskFor`); Tyre (ID: `place:Tyre`)

## Psalms 46:1 (id: 24557)

*According to Alamoth* (“maidens”; see also study note on 1 Chronicles 15:20). In worship, a choir or a group of instrumentalists performed this hymn.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 46:1
* **Associated Articles:** 1 Chronicles 15:20 (ID: `13532@TyndaleStudyNotes`)

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Refuge (ID: `keyterm:Refuge`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 46:1–3 (id: 24555)

Even during natural disasters, the citizens of Zion show no fear.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 46:1-3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Refuge (ID: `keyterm:Refuge`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 46:1–11 (id: 24554)

This psalm of Zion celebrates God's special presence in Jerusalem (see also [Psalms 48](https://ref.ly/Ps48:1-Ps48:14), [76](https://ref.ly/Ps76:1-Ps76:12), [87](https://ref.ly/Ps87:1-Ps87:7), [122](https://ref.ly/Ps122:1-Ps122:9)). It encourages trust in the Lord because the people of Zion have his protection.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 46:1-11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`); Refuge (ID: `keyterm:Refuge`); Wagon (ID: `realia:Wagon`); Small Shield (ID: `realia:SmallShield`); Bow (ID: `realia:Bow`); Spear (ID: `realia:Spear`)

## Psalms 46:4 (id: 24559)

This river provides life, renewal, and healing like the streams flowing through Eden ([Psalm 36:8–9](https://ref.ly/Ps36:8-Ps36:9); see [Genesis 2:10](https://ref.ly/Gen2:10); [Ezekiel 47](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:1-Ezek47:23); [Revelation 22:2](https://ref.ly/Rev22:2)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 46:4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 46:4–6 (id: 24558)

The strong city, where God lives, protects its people. They enjoy the river flowing within the city. This river is calm, unlike the turbulent waters (representing the powers of the nations) outside the city.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 46:4-6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`)

## Psalms 46:5 (id: 24560)

*when morning dawns*: In the ancient Near East, enemies attacked cities during the day. Even in dangerous times, the Lord will protect the place where his name is.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 46:5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 46:6 (id: 24561)

People and nations live in chaos. They take every chance to benefit themselves ([Psalm 83:2](https://ref.ly/Ps83:2)), stay busy with many things ([39:6](https://ref.ly/Ps39:6)), and fill the world with noise. They are unstable and can fall apart, unlike the city of God (compare [Psalm 15:5](https://ref.ly/Ps15:5)). But when the godly are troubled, they cry out to God for help ([Psalm 42:5](https://ref.ly/Ps42:5), [11](https://ref.ly/Ps42:11); [43:5](https://ref.ly/Ps43:5); [55:17](https://ref.ly/Ps55:17)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 46:6


## Psalms 46:7 (id: 24562)

* This refrain reminds us who the God of Zion is.
* *with us*: Hebrew *'immanu,* part of the name Immanuel, meaning “God with us” ([Isaiah 7:14](https://ref.ly/Isa7:14); [8:8](https://ref.ly/Isa8:8); [Matthew 1:23](https://ref.ly/Matt1:23)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 46:7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 46:8 (id: 24564)

Israel witnessed the Lord's amazing works in the wilderness ([Psalms 107:24](https://ref.ly/Ps107:24); [111:2](https://ref.ly/Ps111:2)), but the wicked, who do not care about them ([28:5](https://ref.ly/Ps28:5)), end up desolate ([73:19](https://ref.ly/Ps73:19)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 46:8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 46:8–9 (id: 24563)

This invitation to trust in the Lord offers a perspective on God’s interactions with human powers.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 46:8-9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Wagon (ID: `realia:Wagon`); Small Shield (ID: `realia:SmallShield`); Bow (ID: `realia:Bow`); Spear (ID: `realia:Spear`)

## Psalms 46:10 (id: 24556)

* A message directly from the Lord confirms the truth of the psalmist’s vision (see also [Psalms 12:5–6](https://ref.ly/Ps12:5-Ps12:6); [32:8](https://ref.ly/Ps32:8)).
* *know that I am God*: The Lord will fulfill his promises ([Isaiah 45:3](https://ref.ly/Isa45:3); [49:23](https://ref.ly/Isa49:23); [60:16](https://ref.ly/Isa60:16)).
* God will be honored ([Psalm 47](https://ref.ly/Ps47:1-Ps47:9); [Isaiah 6:1](https://ref.ly/Isa6:1)); he will judge the nations and save his people (see [Psalms 57:11](https://ref.ly/Ps57:11); [89:13](https://ref.ly/Ps89:13); [108:5–6](https://ref.ly/Ps108:5-Ps108:6); [118:16](https://ref.ly/Ps118:16)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 46:10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 47:1 (id: 24567)

People clap their hands in praise and recognition of wonderful deeds ([Psalm 47:3–4](https://ref.ly/Ps47:3-Ps47:4); see [98:8](https://ref.ly/Ps98:8); [Isaiah 55:12](https://ref.ly/Isa55:12); compare [Nahum 3:19](https://ref.ly/Nah3:19)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 47:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 47:1–2 (id: 24565)

As the ruler of all nations, God deserves praise.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 47:1-2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`)

## Psalms 47:1–9 (id: 24566)

This psalm praises God's rule as king over the entire earth. It describes God's victory with Israel and other nations. Both Israel ([Psalm 47:3–4](https://ref.ly/Ps47:3-Ps47:4)) and the nations ([47:9](https://ref.ly/Ps47:9)) are part of God's kingdom.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 47:1-9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Abraham (ID: `person:Abraham`); To Cause to Possess (ID: `keyterm:Possess`); Throne (ID: `realia:Throne`); Small Shield (ID: `realia:SmallShield`); Rams Horn (ID: `realia:RamsHorn`)

## Psalms 47:3–4 (id: 24568)

The Lord gave Israel victory, especially under David (see [Psalm 18:40–50](https://ref.ly/Ps18:40-Ps18:50)). God's powerful acts of redemption show his love for Israel.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 47:3-4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** To Cause to Possess (ID: `keyterm:Possess`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`)

## Psalms 47:4 (id: 24569)

* *pride of Jacob*: Jacob was the father of all Israelites ([Genesis 32:28](https://ref.ly/Gen32:28)). The name *Jacob* often represents the nation of Israel.
* God loves and commits himself to Jacob (see [Psalms 78:68](https://ref.ly/Ps78:68); [127:2](https://ref.ly/Ps127:2); see [Romans 11:28](https://ref.ly/Rom11:28)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 47:4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** To Cause to Possess (ID: `keyterm:Possess`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`)

## Psalms 47:5 (id: 24571)

*horn*: Or *rams’ horns* (see [Psalm 81:3](https://ref.ly/Ps81:3)). People sometimes blew rams’ horns during celebrations ([Psalm 98:5–6](https://ref.ly/Ps98:5-Ps98:6); [2 Kings 11:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:14); [1 Chronicles 15:28](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:28)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 47:5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Rams Horn (ID: `realia:RamsHorn`)

## Psalms 47:5–6 (id: 24570)

The psalmist describes the Lord as returning to heaven, having accomplished his victories. For a description of his descent, see [Psalm 18:9–16](https://ref.ly/Ps18:9-Ps18:16).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 47:5-6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Rams Horn (ID: `realia:RamsHorn`)

## Psalms 47:7 (id: 24572)

People from all over the world (see [Psalm 96:1–3](https://ref.ly/Ps96:1-Ps96:3)) will worship God.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 47:7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 48:1–14 (id: 24573)

This song of Zion (see also [Psalms 46](https://ref.ly/Ps46:1-Ps46:11), [76](https://ref.ly/Ps76:1-Ps76:12), [87](https://ref.ly/Ps87:1-Ps87:7), [122](https://ref.ly/Ps122:1-Ps122:9)) clearly describes Jerusalem (Zion) as the city of the Great King, where the godly find protection ([48:1–3](https://ref.ly/Ps48:1-Ps48:3)). God rules from Zion with faithfulness, righteousness, and justice, inspiring his people with confidence and joy ([Psalm 48:9–11](https://ref.ly/Ps48:9-Ps48:11)). They dedicate themselves to witnessing Zion's glory so they can share it with the next generation ([Psalm 48:8](https://ref.ly/Ps48:8), [12–14](https://ref.ly/Ps48:12-Ps48:14)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 48:1-14

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); Tarshish (ID: `place:Tarshish`); Judah (ID: `person:Judah.4`); Tribe of Judah (ID: `group:Judah`); Zaphon (ID: `place:Zaphon`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Tower (ID: `realia:Tower`); Ship (ID: `realia:Ship`)

## Psalms 48:3 (id: 24576)

God lives in Zion to protect it from all enemies (see [Psalm 46:5](https://ref.ly/Ps46:5)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 48:3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 48:7 (id: 24577)

The ships of Tarshish were built for long voyages and represent human achievement (see also [2 Chronicles 9:21](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:21); [Isaiah 2:16](https://ref.ly/Isa2:16)). Their destruction might be the event mentioned in [1 Kings 22:48](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:48).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 48:7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Tarshish (ID: `place:Tarshish`); Ship (ID: `realia:Ship`)

## Psalms 48:9–11 (id: 24578)

The psalmist happily celebrates the Lord's presence with the community, thinking about God's faithful, righteous, and just rule.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 48:9-11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Judah (ID: `person:Judah.4`); Tribe of Judah (ID: `group:Judah`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`)

## Psalms 48:12–13 (id: 24574)

*towers … ramparts … citadel*: These structures show Zion's strength.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 48:12-13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); Tower (ID: `realia:Tower`)

## Psalms 48:14 (id: 24575)

No one is like God ([Psalms 35:10](https://ref.ly/Ps35:10); [71:19](https://ref.ly/Ps71:19); [86:8](https://ref.ly/Ps86:8); [89:6](https://ref.ly/Ps89:6), [8](https://ref.ly/Ps89:8); [113:5](https://ref.ly/Ps113:5); see [Isaiah 40:18](https://ref.ly/Isa40:18), [25](https://ref.ly/Isa40:25)). He is the shepherd King ([Psalm 95:7](https://ref.ly/Ps95:7)) who will guide (see [5:8](https://ref.ly/Ps5:8); [23:2](https://ref.ly/Ps23:2)) the sheep of his flock ([77:20](https://ref.ly/Ps77:20)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 48:14

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 49:1–2 (id: 24580)

Anyone who will listen can gain wisdom ([Proverbs 1:5](https://ref.ly/Prov1:5), [8](https://ref.ly/Prov1:8), [33](https://ref.ly/Prov1:33)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 49:1-2


## Psalms 49:1–4 (id: 24581)

The wisdom teacher invites everyone to come and learn wisdom. He promises to teach them through a proverb and a riddle.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 49:1-4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Understanding (ID: `keyterm:Understanding`); Proverb (ID: `keyterm:Saying`); Lute (ID: `realia:Lute`)

## Psalms 49:1–20 (id: 24579)

In this wisdom psalm, a wise teacher warns about enemies who live richly and care only for themselves ([Psalm 49:5–9](https://ref.ly/Ps49:5-Ps49:9)). They cannot save anyone, not even themselves, from death or being forgotten ([Psalm 49:10–12](https://ref.ly/Ps49:10-Ps49:12)). Like fattened animals, they are led to slaughter ([Psalm 49:14–20](https://ref.ly/Ps49:14-Ps49:20)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 49:1-20

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Hell (ID: `place:Hell`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); Redeem (ID: `keyterm:Redeem`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Stupid (ID: `keyterm:Stupid`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Understanding (ID: `keyterm:Understanding`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Perceive (ID: `keyterm:Perceive`); Proverb (ID: `keyterm:Saying`); Lute (ID: `realia:Lute`); House (ID: `realia:House`); Sheep (ID: `fauna:Lamb`); Flock (ID: `fauna:Flock`); Goat (ID: `fauna:Goat`)

The teacher used proverbs and riddles to teach wisdom (see [Proverbs 1:5–6](https://ref.ly/Prov1:5-Prov1:6)).

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Proverb (ID: `keyterm:Saying`); Lute (ID: `realia:Lute`)

If enemies who live for material rewards cannot sustain themselves, how can they give life to others?

The whole universe must pay attention when God approaches to bring justice into the world.

## Psalms 50:4–6 (id: 24607)

The Lord is coming to bring justice. This hope encourages his faithful people. However, those who have been unfaithful will not receive blessings when the heavens and earth testify against them (see [Deuteronomy 30:19](https://ref.ly/Deut30:19); [31:28](https://ref.ly/Deut31:28); [32:1](https://ref.ly/Deut32:1); [Isaiah 1:2](https://ref.ly/Isa1:2)). The Lord comes to restore order to the world (see [Psalms 5:8](https://ref.ly/Ps5:8); [22:31](https://ref.ly/Ps22:31); [33:5](https://ref.ly/Ps33:5); [40:9](https://ref.ly/Ps40:9); [50:6](https://ref.ly/Ps50:6); [71:16](https://ref.ly/Ps71:16); [97:6](https://ref.ly/Ps97:6)).

## Psalms 50:7–13 (id: 24608)

The psalmist warns against valuing sacrificial rituals more than having the right attitude in your heart.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 50:7-13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Cattle (ID: `fauna:Bull`); Flock (ID: `fauna:Flock`); Goat (ID: `fauna:Goat`); Slaughtering (ID: `keyterm:Slaughtering`); Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Sheepfold (ID: `realia:Sheepfold`); Sandalwood (ID: `flora:Sandalwood`)

## Psalms 50:9 (id: 24609)

Unlike pagan gods, God does not need food. Instead, he wants his people to serve him with sincere hearts ([Psalm 50:14–15](https://ref.ly/Ps50:14-Ps50:15)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 50:9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Flock (ID: `fauna:Flock`); Sheepfold (ID: `realia:Sheepfold`); Cattle (ID: `fauna:Bull`); Goat (ID: `fauna:Goat`)

## Psalms 50:10 (id: 24599)

*the cattle on a thousand hills*: God has no needs because he owns everything.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 50:10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Sandalwood (ID: `flora:Sandalwood`)

## Psalms 50:14 (id: 24600)

* Thankfulness involves recognizing and appreciating God's blessings beyond personal gain (see [Psalms 51:15–17](https://ref.ly/Ps51:15-Ps51:17); [54:6](https://ref.ly/Ps54:6)).
* True sacrifice means dedicating oneself to honor God ([Psalm 50:23](https://ref.ly/Ps50:23); [Romans 12:1–2](https://ref.ly/Rom12:1-Rom12:2)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 50:14

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Vow (ID: `keyterm:Vow`); Praise (ID: `keyterm:Praise`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 50:15

## Psalms 50:16–21 (id: 24602)

This message is against the wicked, who only pretend to listen to God.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 50:16-21

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Decree (ID: `keyterm:Decree`); Deceit (ID: `keyterm:Deceit`); Covenant (ID: `keyterm:Covenant`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 50:17 (id: 24603)

*My instruction … My words*: The wicked may observe rituals, but they reject more important matters such as justice, mercy, and faithfulness (compare [Matthew 23:23–24](https://ref.ly/Matt23:23-Matt23:24)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 50:17


## Psalms 50:21 (id: 24604)

Because he is patient, God remained silent.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 50:21

God will destroy the wicked like a lion tearing its prey ([Isaiah 5:29](https://ref.ly/Isa5:29); [31:4](https://ref.ly/Isa31:4); [Ezekiel 19:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:3); compare [Psalms 7:2](https://ref.ly/Ps7:2); [22:13](https://ref.ly/Ps22:13)).

The wicked will experience God’s justice, while the godly will see God’s everlasting salvation.

## Psalms 51:1 (id: 24620)

A prayer for God's mercy and kindness is a type of lament. Sometimes, the psalmist asks for mercy because he recognizes his own weakness and sin (see also [Psalm 6:2](https://ref.ly/Ps6:2); [9:13](https://ref.ly/Ps9:13); [25:16](https://ref.ly/Ps25:16); [31:9](https://ref.ly/Ps31:9); [41:4](https://ref.ly/Ps41:4), [10](https://ref.ly/Ps41:10)). Other times, the plea comes from the psalmist's dedication to the Lord (see [Psalm 26:11](https://ref.ly/Ps26:11); [27:7](https://ref.ly/Ps27:7); [119:29](https://ref.ly/Ps119:29), [58](https://ref.ly/Ps119:58), [132](https://ref.ly/Ps119:132)). In every situation, God's mercy leads to inner renewal.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 51:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Psalm (ID: `keyterm:Psalm`); Forgive (ID: `keyterm:Forgive`); David (ID: `person:David`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); To Be Kindly Disposed (ID: `keyterm:KindlyDisposed`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`)

## Psalms 51:1–2 (id: 24611)

The person who regrets their wrongdoing cannot offer anything to God. They need God's favor, mercy, forgiveness, and blessing to experience renewal and restoration.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 51:1-2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Psalm (ID: `keyterm:Psalm`); Pure (ID: `keyterm:Pure`); To Sin (ID: `keyterm:Sin`); Forgive (ID: `keyterm:Forgive`); David (ID: `person:David`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); To Be Kindly Disposed (ID: `keyterm:KindlyDisposed`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`)

## Psalms 51:1–19 (id: 24610)

This heartfelt prayer for restoration seeks God's favor, mercy, forgiveness, and cleansing. With a broken spirit, the psalmist admits his sin and takes responsibility ([Psalm 51:3–6](https://ref.ly/Ps51:3-Ps51:6)). He then asks God to remove his guilt and renew him internally ([Psalm 51:7–12](https://ref.ly/Ps51:7-Ps51:12)). The psalmist commits again to living wisely and joyfully while serving God and others ([Psalm 51:13–17](https://ref.ly/Ps51:13-Ps51:17)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 51:1-19

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); To Sin (ID: `keyterm:Sin`); Pure (ID: `keyterm:Pure`); Slaughtering (ID: `keyterm:Slaughtering`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); Forgive (ID: `keyterm:Forgive`); To Cleanse (ID: `keyterm:Cleanse.2`); Psalm (ID: `keyterm:Psalm`); Delight (ID: `keyterm:Delight`); David (ID: `person:David`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Be (Or Show Oneself) Just (ID: `keyterm:Just`); The Way (ID: `keyterm:TheWay`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); To Be Kindly Disposed (ID: `keyterm:KindlyDisposed`); Ability (ID: `keyterm:Ability`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); Tabernacle Altar (ID: `realia:TabernacleAltar`); Hyssop (ID: `flora:Hyssop`); Cattle (ID: `fauna:Bull`); Stone Altar (ID: `realia:StoneAltar`); Altar (ID: `realia:Altar`); Temple Altar (ID: `realia:TempleAltar`)

## Psalms 51:1–65.13 (id: 24612)

These psalms of David share a common theme. They reflect on the experience of evil. In [Psalm 51](https://ref.ly/Ps51:1-Ps51:19), the psalmist admits his wrongdoing and asks for God's forgiveness. [Psalms 52–64](https://ref.ly/Ps52:1-Ps64:10) express sorrow over specific evils David faced. A song of praise ([Psalm 65](https://ref.ly/Ps65:1-Ps65:13)) brings David's laments to an end.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 51:1-65:13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); To Sin (ID: `keyterm:Sin`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Deceit (ID: `keyterm:Deceit`); Vow (ID: `keyterm:Vow`); Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); Arrow (ID: `keyterm:Arrow`); To Be Kindly Disposed (ID: `keyterm:KindlyDisposed`); Pure (ID: `keyterm:Pure`); Slaughtering (ID: `keyterm:Slaughtering`); Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`); Forgive (ID: `keyterm:Forgive`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); Refuge (ID: `keyterm:Refuge`); Sword (ID: `realia:Sword`); Arrow (ID: `realia:Arrow`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); Appoint (ID: `keyterm:Appoint`); Have Insight (ID: `keyterm:HaveInsight`); Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); Hell (ID: `place:Hell`)

## Psalms 51:2 (id: 24621)

*Wash me … cleanse me*: The psalmist asks for cleansing and freedom from the guilt of his sin ([Psalm 51:7](https://ref.ly/Ps51:7); see [26:6](https://ref.ly/Ps26:6); [Isaiah 1:16](https://ref.ly/Isa1:16), [18](https://ref.ly/Isa1:18); [4:4](https://ref.ly/Isa4:4)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 51:2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Pure (ID: `keyterm:Pure`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); To Sin (ID: `keyterm:Sin`)

## Psalms 51:3–6 (id: 24622)

The psalmist accepts the results of his sin against God. He understands that he has no goodness without God's renewal.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 51:3-6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** To Sin (ID: `keyterm:Sin`); Be (Or Show Oneself) Just (ID: `keyterm:Just`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Ability (ID: `keyterm:Ability`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`)

## Psalms 51:4 (id: 24623)

*Against You, You only*: This is an exaggeration. The psalmist knows he also sinned against people ([2 Samuel 11:2–4](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:2-2Sam11:4), [15–17](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:15-2Sam11:17)), but his sin against God is more important. He expects God to rebuke him and will accept whatever verdict God gives (see [Romans 3:4](https://ref.ly/Rom3:4)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 51:4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** To Sin (ID: `keyterm:Sin`); Be (Or Show Oneself) Just (ID: `keyterm:Just`)

## Psalms 51:5 (id: 24624)

*I was brought forth in iniquity*: The psalmist is not making excuses but is admitting how deep his sinfulness is. From the time we are conceived*,* we all share in the human condition of sinfulness.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 51:5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`)

## Psalms 51:7 (id: 24625)

*Purify me with hyssop* (literally *Purify me with the hyssop branch*; see [Exodus 12:22](https://ref.ly/Exod12:22); [Leviticus 14:4](https://ref.ly/Lev14:4); [John 19:29](https://ref.ly/John19:29); [Hebrews 9:19](https://ref.ly/Heb9:19)): The psalmist relies on God to cleanse and renew him (see [Isaiah 1:18](https://ref.ly/Isa1:18)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 51:7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** To Cleanse (ID: `keyterm:Cleanse.2`); Pure (ID: `keyterm:Pure`); Hyssop (ID: `flora:Hyssop`)

## Psalms 51:8 (id: 24626)

* Regaining joy is a gracious gift from God.
* *You have crushed*: The psalmist feels completely broken ([Psalm 6:2](https://ref.ly/Ps6:2); compare [34:20](https://ref.ly/Ps34:20)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 51:8

God’s Spirit renews and recreates the human spirit.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 51:11

## Psalms 52:1 (id: 24630)

The accuser, Doeg the Edomite, is an example of a person who chose to do evil.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 52:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 52:1–4 (id: 24628)

Liars deceive themselves by thinking they can be heroes with their words. They see themselves as innocent, even though they love evil and cause harm. Those who speak curses, sarcasm, bitterness, arrogant boasting, and lies ([Psalms 31:18](https://ref.ly/Ps31:18); [34:13](https://ref.ly/Ps34:13); [59:7](https://ref.ly/Ps59:7), [12](https://ref.ly/Ps59:12); [120:2–3](https://ref.ly/Ps120:2-Ps120:3); [140:3](https://ref.ly/Ps140:3), [9](https://ref.ly/Ps140:9)) create chaos, isolation, and destruction ([James 3:5](https://ref.ly/Jas3:5)). Their sharp words cut like swords and daggers ([Psalms 55:21](https://ref.ly/Ps55:21); [57:4](https://ref.ly/Ps57:4); [64:3](https://ref.ly/Ps64:3)), the fangs of a snake ([140:3](https://ref.ly/Ps140:3)), or a razor.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 52:1-4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Deceit (ID: `keyterm:Deceit`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Razor (ID: `realia:Razor`)

## Psalms 52:1–9 (id: 24629)

Wicked people use words to destroy relationships and communities. They foolishly think that they can be heroes by their words ([Psalm 52:1–4](https://ref.ly/Ps52:1-Ps52:4)), but their downfall comes suddenly ([52:5–7](https://ref.ly/Ps52:5-Ps52:7)). In contrast, the godly renew their commitment to the Lord ([Psalm 52:8–9](https://ref.ly/Ps52:8-Ps52:9)) and find security in their future with him.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 52:1-9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Deceit (ID: `keyterm:Deceit`); Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Loyal Person (ID: `keyterm:LoyalPerson`); House (ID: `realia:House`); Fortress (ID: `realia:Fortress`); Stone Pine (ID: `flora:Pine`); Olive Tree (ID: `flora:OliveTree`); Razor (ID: `realia:Razor`)

Dishonest speech can change a person's character.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 52:3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Love (ID: `keyterm:Love`)

God will strike the wicked down like a pagan altar, idol, or unclean vessel ([Exodus 34:13](https://ref.ly/Exod34:13); [Leviticus 11:35](https://ref.ly/Lev11:35); [2 Kings 10:27](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:27)).

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Fortress (ID: `realia:Fortress`)

## Psalms 54:1–7 (id: 24639)

This personal lament was caused by violent and godless people ([Psalm 54:3](https://ref.ly/Ps54:3)). The psalmist prays to God and focuses on God's help ([54:4](https://ref.ly/Ps54:4)). ## Psalms 54:3 (id: 24641)

* The strangers were excluded from the covenant community (see [Psalm 109:11](https://ref.ly/Ps109:11); [Proverbs 5:10](https://ref.ly/Prov5:10)).
* Proud and violent people do not respect God or others (see [Psalms 35:11](https://ref.ly/Ps35:11), [25](https://ref.ly/Ps35:25); [86:14](https://ref.ly/Ps86:14); [Proverbs 11:16](https://ref.ly/Prov11:16)).

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Mighty (ID: `keyterm:Mighty.2`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

A voluntary offering is a sacrifice of praise. Sacrifices of praise are the kind of offering God most desires (see [Psalm 50:14–15](https://ref.ly/Ps50:14-Ps50:15), [23](https://ref.ly/Ps50:23)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 54:6

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 55:1-3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); Plea for Mercy (ID: `keyterm:Supplication`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 55:1–23 (id: 24644)

The psalmist faces enemies led by a former friend ([Psalm 55:12–14](https://ref.ly/Ps55:12-Ps55:14)). This causes him great distress. He prays to the Lord and trusts him for justice ([Psalm 55:22–23](https://ref.ly/Ps55:22-Ps55:23)). 

Jesus went through similar pain when Judas, his friend, betrayed him. He also experienced the psalmist’s prayer being answered: 

1. by taking the place of the wicked and receiving their punishment ([Isaiah 53:7–9](https://ref.ly/Isa53:7-Isa53:9)), and
2. by trusting the Father, who saved ([Luke 23:46](https://ref.ly/Luke23:46)) and rewarded him ([Isaiah 53:10–12](https://ref.ly/Isa53:10-Isa53:12)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 55:1-23

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Hell (ID: `place:Hell`); Peace (ID: `keyterm:IntactState`); Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); Deceit (ID: `keyterm:Deceit`); Terror (ID: `keyterm:Terror`); To Dishonor (ID: `keyterm:Dishonor`); Covenant (ID: `keyterm:Covenant`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Redeem (ID: `keyterm:Redeem`); Plea for Mercy (ID: `keyterm:Supplication`); Anointing Oil (ID: `realia:AnointingOil`); Sword (ID: `realia:Sword`); House (ID: `realia:House`); Dove (ID: `fauna:Dove`); Olive Oil (ID: `realia:OliveOil`)

All these words point to the short and limited nature of human life.

## Psalms 57:2 (id: 24661)

* *God Most High* (Hebrew *Elohim\-Elyon*) is a common title for God. He is more powerful than all real or imagined supernatural beings (see [Psalms 91:1](https://ref.ly/Ps91:1); [97:9](https://ref.ly/Ps97:9); [Genesis 14:18–22](https://ref.ly/Gen14:18-Gen14:22)).
* *who fulfills His purpose for me*: Or *who will end my \[trouble]*, or *who deals kindly with me*.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 57:2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 57:3–4 (id: 24662)

These verses are a prayer for rescue from strong enemies. The metaphor of fierce lions describes human foes. The psalmist feels unable to escape them without God's help (see also [Psalms 35:11–21](https://ref.ly/Ps35:11-Ps35:21); [124:6](https://ref.ly/Ps124:6)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 57:3-4

## Psalms 58:1 (id: 24670)

*rulers* (or *gods*): People used this term for high officials and administrators of justice ([Psalm 82:1](https://ref.ly/Ps82:1), [6](https://ref.ly/Ps82:6)). In other contexts, it might refer to angelic creatures ([Psalm 89:7](https://ref.ly/Ps89:7)) or the gods of other nations ([82:1](https://ref.ly/Ps82:1); [Daniel 11:36](https://ref.ly/Dan11:36)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 58:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Fairness (ID: `keyterm:Fairness`)

## Psalms 58:1–5 (id: 24668)

The administrators of justice were furthering their own interests rather than those of the needy. The absence of justice gradually resulted in a violent society.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 58:1-5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Fairness (ID: `keyterm:Fairness`); Cobra (ID: `fauna:Cobra`); Snake (ID: `fauna:Snake`)

## Psalms 58:1–11 (id: 24667)

The administration of justice is a mark of good government. God expects nothing less from his people. The psalmist condemns Israel’s leaders for abusing their power, and he calls on the Lord to rain down his curses upon the wicked. The demonstration of divine justice will reassure the godly that God is just.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 58:1-11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Fairness (ID: `keyterm:Fairness`); Men (ID: `group:GenericMales`); Arrow (ID: `keyterm:Arrow`); Perceive (ID: `keyterm:Perceive`); Boxthorn (ID: `flora:Boxthorn`); Snail (ID: `fauna:Snail`); Cobra (ID: `fauna:Cobra`); Cooking Pot (ID: `realia:CookingPot`); Snake (ID: `fauna:Snake`); Arrow (ID: `realia:Arrow`); Lion (ID: `fauna:Lion`)

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Edom (ID: `place:Edom`); Fortification (ID: `realia:Fortification`)

## Psalms 61:1–8 (id: 24702)

The psalmist asks God to protect him and guide him into his presence ([Psalm 61:4–5](https://ref.ly/Ps61:4-Ps61:5)). He prays for God to extend and protect the king's rule and promises to praise God.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 61:1-8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Vow (ID: `keyterm:Vow`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`); Refuge (ID: `keyterm:Refuge`); To Cause to Possess (ID: `keyterm:Possess`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Tower (ID: `realia:Tower`)

## Psalms 61:1–63.11 (id: 24701)

In these royal prayers, the psalmist longs for God’s presence. All three psalms concern themselves with the king’s security.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 61:1-63:11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Refuge (ID: `keyterm:Refuge`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Vow (ID: `keyterm:Vow`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`); Swear (ID: `keyterm:Swear`); To Cause to Possess (ID: `keyterm:Possess`); Truth (ID: `keyterm:Truth`); Hope (ID: `keyterm:Hope`); Balance (ID: `realia:Balance`); Jackal (ID: `fauna:Jackal`); Tower (ID: `realia:Tower`)

## Psalms 61:2 (id: 24703)

* *the ends of the earth*: This phrase suggests a cry that comes from a place far away from God.
* The psalmist feels deep sorrow and distress.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 61:2


## Psalms 61:4 (id: 24704)

* The poet desires God's presence and seeks entry into God's earthly home for protection and care. Being welcomed into God's sanctuary (*tent*) is the greatest blessing (see [Psalms 15:1](https://ref.ly/Ps15:1); [23:6](https://ref.ly/Ps23:6); [24:3–6](https://ref.ly/Ps24:3-Ps24:6)). The Hebrew word for *tent* refers to the tabernacle and the tent David built for the ark ([1 Chronicles 15:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:1)). See also study note on John 1:14.
* The shelter of your wings represents God's protection and care (see [Psalms 9:9](https://ref.ly/Ps9:9); [17:8](https://ref.ly/Ps17:8)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 61:4
* **Associated Articles:** John 1:14 (ID: `18788@TyndaleStudyNotes`)

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`)

## Psalms 61:5 (id: 24705)

* *You have heard my vows*: The psalmist's vows might be part of ceremonial sacrifices ([Psalms 66:13–15](https://ref.ly/Ps66:13-Ps66:15); [76:11](https://ref.ly/Ps76:11); [116:14](https://ref.ly/Ps116:14), [18](https://ref.ly/Ps116:18)). He praises and thanks God for rescuing him.
* The inheritance refers to the promise of the land (see [Psalm 60](https://ref.ly/Ps60:1-Ps60:12)), the sacredness of the temple, the joy of God's presence ([Psalm 119:111](https://ref.ly/Ps119:111)), or any similar reward ([Psalms 16:6](https://ref.ly/Ps16:6); [37:9](https://ref.ly/Ps37:9), [11](https://ref.ly/Ps37:11), [18](https://ref.ly/Ps37:18), [22](https://ref.ly/Ps37:22), [29](https://ref.ly/Ps37:29), [34](https://ref.ly/Ps37:34); [127:3](https://ref.ly/Ps127:3)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 61:5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** To Cause to Possess (ID: `keyterm:Possess`); Vow (ID: `keyterm:Vow`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 62:1 (id: 24709)

See [1 Chronicles 25:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:1); also in [Psalms 39](https://ref.ly/Ps39:1-Ps39:13) and [77](https://ref.ly/Ps77:1-Ps77:20).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 62:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 62:1–12 (id: 24706)

This psalm expresses trust in the king and includes a prayer for him. The king relies on God even during tough times. 

His tricky and strong enemies challenge him, but he stays strong. He urges himself and his people to trust God. 

He wants them to view their human enemies from God's perspective, where they seem weak, temporary, and deceitful ([Psalm 62:9](https://ref.ly/Ps62:9)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 62:1-12

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Refuge (ID: `keyterm:Refuge`); Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Hope (ID: `keyterm:Hope`); Balance (ID: `realia:Balance`)

## Psalms 62:3–4 (id: 24710)

The enemies want to overthrow the king using lies and tricks.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 62:3-4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`)

## Psalms 62:4 (id: 24711)

*his lofty perch:* This means the royal throne.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 62:4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`)

## Psalms 62:5–8 (id: 24712)

The psalmist encourages himself and others to place their hope in God.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 62:5-8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Refuge (ID: `keyterm:Refuge`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); Hope (ID: `keyterm:Hope`)

## Psalms 62:9 (id: 24714)

*are but a lie*: People often try to appear stronger and better than they truly are.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 62:9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Balance (ID: `realia:Balance`)

## Psalms 62:9–12 (id: 24713)

Humans are temporary, unlike God. Even when successful, their prosperity does not last long.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 62:9-12

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`); Balance (ID: `realia:Balance`)

## Psalms 62:10 (id: 24707)

*Extortion* involves using threats to force someone to give up their belongings. *Stealing* means taking what belongs to someone else.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 62:10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Trust (ID: `keyterm:LeanOn`)

## Psalms 62:11–12 (id: 24708)

Only God balances power and loving devotion. He cares for his people by removing those who do evil from the world (see [Psalm 1:6](https://ref.ly/Ps1:6)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 62:11-12

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`)

## Psalms 63:1 (id: 24717)

* Together, *soul* and *body* refer to a person's entire being.
* *dry and weary land*: When David lived in the desert, he felt spiritually empty, away from God's people and the sanctuary.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 63:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 63:1–11 (id: 24715)

The king deeply desires God's presence. He experiences God's goodness in everything he does. Despite being tired and troubled by the wicked, the king finds strength in God and praises him. God is his source of life ([Psalm 63:3](https://ref.ly/Ps63:3)), while the wicked face destruction ([63:9](https://ref.ly/Ps63:9)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 63:1-11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); Swear (ID: `keyterm:Swear`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Jackal (ID: `fauna:Jackal`)

## Psalms 63:2 (id: 24718)

The psalmist remembers that he had found God to be present in his sanctuary (see [Psalm 18:6](https://ref.ly/Ps18:6); [61:4](https://ref.ly/Ps61:4); [62:5](https://ref.ly/Ps62:5), [11–12](https://ref.ly/Ps62:11-Ps62:12)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 63:2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`)

## Psalms 63:3 (id: 24719)

*better than life*: The best life comes from a relationship with God (see [Psalms 4:7](https://ref.ly/Ps4:7); [63:5](https://ref.ly/Ps63:5); [Proverbs 16:8](https://ref.ly/Prov16:8)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 63:3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`)

## Psalms 63:6 (id: 24721)

Meditating on God requires focus, concentration, thought, and reflection (see [Psalm 1:2](https://ref.ly/Ps1:2)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 63:6


## Psalms 63:6–8 (id: 24720)

The psalmist thinks about and feels God's presence during the night. When it is dark, God is the light. The quiet night and the wait for morning offer time to reflect on the true meaning of life with the Lord.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 63:6-8


## Psalms 63:9 (id: 24722)

* The wicked planned to ruin the psalmist's life with lies and deceit, but they would fail.
* *the depths of the earth*: This refers to the place of the dead.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 63:9


## Psalms 63:10 (id: 24716)

Wild foxes and jackals are often associated with places that are ruined and abandoned ([Isaiah 13:20–22](https://ref.ly/Isa13:20-Isa13:22); [34:10–15](https://ref.ly/Isa34:10-Isa34:15); [Jeremiah 9:11](https://ref.ly/Jer9:11); [49:33](https://ref.ly/Jer49:33); [51:37](https://ref.ly/Jer51:37); [Lamentations 5:18](https://ref.ly/Lam5:18); [Malachi 1:3](https://ref.ly/Mal1:3)). The wicked will not receive honor in death. Scavenging animals will consume their bodies ([Ecclesiastes 6:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl6:3); [Jeremiah 22:18–19](https://ref.ly/Jer22:18-Jer22:19); [36:29–30](https://ref.ly/Jer36:29-Jer36:30)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 63:10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Jackal (ID: `fauna:Jackal`)

## Psalms 64:1–6 (id: 24724)

These verses form a prayer for rescue from bold and powerful liars.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 64:1-6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Complete (ID: `keyterm:Complete`); Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Arrow (ID: `keyterm:Arrow`); Sword (ID: `realia:Sword`); Arrow (ID: `realia:Arrow`); Trap (ID: `realia:Trap`)

## Psalms 64:1–10 (id: 24723)

This lament speaks against the destructive plans and arrogant attitude of the wicked ([Psalm 64:1–6](https://ref.ly/Ps64:1-Ps64:6)). The psalmist prays to God and finds comfort in God's justice because the righteous judge will undo the wicked's actions. The godly will rejoice and gain wisdom as they think about God's powerful deeds.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 64:1-10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Arrow (ID: `keyterm:Arrow`); Arrow (ID: `realia:Arrow`); Complete (ID: `keyterm:Complete`); Have Insight (ID: `keyterm:HaveInsight`); Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`); Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); Sword (ID: `realia:Sword`); Trap (ID: `realia:Trap`)

## Psalms 64:3 (id: 24725)

*their tongues … bitter words*: Liars use their words to harm others. This description might also refer to practicing magic.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 64:3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Arrow (ID: `keyterm:Arrow`); Sword (ID: `realia:Sword`); Arrow (ID: `realia:Arrow`)

## Psalms 64:6 (id: 24726)

* The enemies make a plan to harm the innocent without getting caught.
* Human beings can act with extreme evil through words, actions, or plans ([Psalm 83:3](https://ref.ly/Ps83:3); see [Jeremiah 17:9](https://ref.ly/Jer17:9)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 64:6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Complete (ID: `keyterm:Complete`)

## Psalms 64:7–10 (id: 24727)

God acts righteously. God stops wickedness and gives the wicked what they intended for others.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 64:7-10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Have Insight (ID: `keyterm:HaveInsight`); Seek Refugue (ID: `keyterm:SeekfindRefuge`); Arrow (ID: `keyterm:Arrow`); Arrow (ID: `realia:Arrow`)

## Psalms 65:1 (id: 24731)

God alone deserves praise and is worthy to have vows made to him.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 65:1

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Vow (ID: `keyterm:Vow`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 65:1–3 (id: 24729)

Praise is the right response to God's great acts in redemption ([Psalm 65:3–5](https://ref.ly/Ps65:3-Ps65:5)) and in nature ([65:5–13](https://ref.ly/Ps65:5-Ps65:13)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 65:1-3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Vow (ID: `keyterm:Vow`); Forgive (ID: `keyterm:Forgive`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`)

## Psalms 65:1–13 (id: 24728)

This psalm recounts the reasons why all people should praise and revere God for his almighty power and merciful care for his universe.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 65:1-13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Appoint (ID: `keyterm:Appoint`); Vow (ID: `keyterm:Vow`); Safety (ID: `keyterm:Safety`); Strength (ID: `keyterm:Strength`); Forgive (ID: `keyterm:Forgive`); Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); Miraculous Sign (ID: `keyterm:MiraculousSign`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Grain (ID: `flora:Grain`); House (ID: `realia:House`); Flock (ID: `fauna:Flock`); Goat (ID: `fauna:Goat`)

## Psalms 65:2 (id: 24732)

*all people*: This means all humans.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 65:2


## Psalms 65:3 (id: 24733)

*You atone*: God graciously removes the guilt of sin (see [Psalm 32:5](https://ref.ly/Ps32:5)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 65:3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Passion (ID: `keyterm:Ardour`); Rebel (ID: `keyterm:Rebel`); Forgive (ID: `keyterm:Forgive`)

## Psalms 65:4 (id: 24734)

* *the one You choose and bring near*: This includes people from Israel and other nations.
* Only those God sees as blameless live in his holy courts ([Psalms 15](https://ref.ly/Ps15:1-Ps15:5) and [24](https://ref.ly/Ps24:1-Ps24:10)).
* At the temple*,* God's earthly palace, all nations can gather to experience God's holy presence. No structure can separate humans from God (see [Revelation 21:22](https://ref.ly/Rev21:22)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 65:4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Holy (ID: `keyterm:Holy`); House (ID: `realia:House`)

## Psalms 65:6–7 (id: 24735)

* God brought order to the mountains*,* the roaring oceans*,* and the pounding waves (see [Psalms 95:4–5](https://ref.ly/Ps95:4-Ps95:5); [104:6–9](https://ref.ly/Ps104:6-Ps104:9)).
* The psalmist compares the chaotic forces of nature with the rebellion of the nations (see [Psalms 2:1–12](https://ref.ly/Ps2:1-Ps2:12); [33:6–11](https://ref.ly/Ps33:6-Ps33:11); [46:6](https://ref.ly/Ps46:6)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 65:6-7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Strength (ID: `keyterm:Strength`)

## Psalms 65:8 (id: 24736)

All over the world ([Job 38:13](https://ref.ly/Job38:13); see [Psalm 50:1–3](https://ref.ly/Ps50:1-Ps50:3)), people should stand in awe of the Lord.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 65:8

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Miraculous Sign (ID: `keyterm:MiraculousSign`)

## Psalms 65:9 (id: 24738)

The *streams of God* and all rivers show God's power and goodness through nature's order and the regularity of the harvests (see [Psalms 1:3](https://ref.ly/Ps1:3); [36:8](https://ref.ly/Ps36:8); [46:4](https://ref.ly/Ps46:4); [Ezekiel 47:6–12](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:6-Ezek47:12); [Zechariah 14:8](https://ref.ly/Zech14:8); [Revelation 22:1](https://ref.ly/Rev22:1)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 65:9

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Appoint (ID: `keyterm:Appoint`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Grain (ID: `flora:Grain`)

## Psalms 65:9–13 (id: 24737)

The created world clearly shows God's power ([Psalm 65:6–8](https://ref.ly/Ps65:6-Ps65:8)). He shows his love by taking care of nature. Everything praises the Lord as all parts work together smoothly.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 65:9-13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Appoint (ID: `keyterm:Appoint`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Grain (ID: `flora:Grain`); Flock (ID: `fauna:Flock`); Goat (ID: `fauna:Goat`)

## Psalms 65:13 (id: 24730)

Nature praises the Creator (see [Psalm 79:13](https://ref.ly/Ps79:13); [96:11–13](https://ref.ly/Ps96:11-Ps96:13); [148:3–5](https://ref.ly/Ps148:3-Ps148:5), [7–12](https://ref.ly/Ps148:7-Ps148:12); [Isaiah 55:12–13](https://ref.ly/Isa55:12-Isa55:13)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 65:13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Flock (ID: `fauna:Flock`); Goat (ID: `fauna:Goat`)

## Psalms 66:1–4 (id: 24740)

The psalmist urges nature to join a powerful song to praise God's glory, name, and power.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 66:1-4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); Psalm (ID: `keyterm:Psalm`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`)

## Psalms 66:1–20 (id: 24739)

This anonymous psalm includes two hymns ([Psalm 66:1–12](https://ref.ly/Ps66:1-Ps66:12), [13–20](https://ref.ly/Ps66:13-Ps66:20)) that praise God for saving his people.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 66:1-20

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Psalm (ID: `keyterm:Psalm`); Vow (ID: `keyterm:Vow`); Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`); Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`); Sheep (ID: `fauna:Lamb`); House (ID: `realia:House`); Cattle (ID: `fauna:Bull`); Net (ID: `realia:Net`); Money (ID: `realia:Money`); Fishnet (ID: `realia:Fishnet`); Flock (ID: `fauna:Flock`); Goat (ID: `fauna:Goat`)

## Psalms 66:2 (id: 24747)

The glory of his name represents the perfection of God’s character.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 66:2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Glory (ID: `keyterm:Glory`)

## Psalms 66:3 (id: 24748)

God defeats his enemies (see [Psalm 18:44](https://ref.ly/Ps18:44); [59:11](https://ref.ly/Ps59:11); [81:15](https://ref.ly/Ps81:15)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 66:3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 66:5–7 (id: 24749)

The story of the exodus and God's victory over the sea causes the people to be amazed ([Psalm 66:5](https://ref.ly/Ps66:5)) and to worship ([66:6](https://ref.ly/Ps66:6)). God performs miracles to redeem people for himself.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 66:5-7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 66:7 (id: 24750)

God's power brings redemption. The same word appears in [Psalm 65:6](https://ref.ly/Ps65:6) to describe God's power in creation (see also [Psalm 80:2](https://ref.ly/Ps80:2)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 66:7


## Psalms 66:8–10 (id: 24751)

The orderly lives of the redeemed reflect the order of God’s world. The Lord protects them from evil and purifies them.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 66:8-10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Money (ID: `realia:Money`)

## Psalms 66:9 (id: 24752)

God guides his people into wisdom and away from foolishness ([Psalms 17:5](https://ref.ly/Ps17:5); [37:31](https://ref.ly/Ps37:31); [121:3](https://ref.ly/Ps121:3)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 66:9


## Psalms 66:10 (id: 24741)

*For You, O God, have tested us*: Like a silversmith heats silver to remove impurities, God's discipline removes sin from his people (see [Psalm 12:6](https://ref.ly/Ps12:6)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 66:10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Money (ID: `realia:Money`)

## Psalms 66:11 (id: 24742)

God decrees and permits the many trying circumstances his people experience.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 66:11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Net (ID: `realia:Net`); Fishnet (ID: `realia:Fishnet`)

## Psalms 66:12 (id: 24743)

*You let men ride over our heads*: Or *You made people rule over us.* This likely refers to a foreign ruler who controlled the Israelites.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 66:12


## Psalms 66:15 (id: 24744)

Vows often included sacrifices of thanksgiving, including burnt offerings (see [Psalms 20:3](https://ref.ly/Ps20:3); [40:6](https://ref.ly/Ps40:6); [50:8](https://ref.ly/Ps50:8); [51:19](https://ref.ly/Ps51:19)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 66:15

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Offering (ID: `keyterm:Offering`); Sheep (ID: `fauna:Lamb`); Cattle (ID: `fauna:Bull`); Flock (ID: `fauna:Flock`); Goat (ID: `fauna:Goat`)

## Psalms 66:16–20 (id: 24745)

The private testimony at the end of the psalm matches the public praise at the beginning ([Psalm 66:1–4](https://ref.ly/Ps66:1-Ps66:4)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 66:16-20

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Kindness (ID: `keyterm:Kindness`)

## Psalms 66:18 (id: 24746)

Confession leads to restoration (see [Psalm 32:5](https://ref.ly/Ps32:5)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 66:18

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Mischief (ID: `keyterm:Mischief`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 67:1–2 (id: 24753)

This prayer is inspired by the priestly blessing in [Numbers 6:24–26](https://ref.ly/Num6:24-Num6:26). God's kindness to Israel influences his reputation among other nations (see [Psalm 96:1–3](https://ref.ly/Ps96:1-Ps96:3)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 67:1-2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** The Way (ID: `keyterm:TheWay`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); To Be Kindly Disposed (ID: `keyterm:KindlyDisposed`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`)

## Psalms 67:1–7 (id: 24754)

This anonymous prayer asks for God's blessing. The psalmist speaks about knowing and worshiping God among all nations (compare [Psalms 64:9](https://ref.ly/Ps64:9); [65:8](https://ref.ly/Ps65:8)). The prayer asks for God's name to remain holy and that his kingdom come (compare [Matthew 6:9–13](https://ref.ly/Matt6:9-Matt6:13)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 67:1-7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Fairness (ID: `keyterm:Fairness`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); The Way (ID: `keyterm:TheWay`); To Be Kindly Disposed (ID: `keyterm:KindlyDisposed`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`)

## Psalms 67:3 (id: 24755)

* God’s international reputation leads to his praise among the nations (see [Psalm 138:4](https://ref.ly/Ps138:4)). Their chaos and rebellion will stop when they join Israel in praising God.
* One God rules over everyone, and the nations will give thanks for God’s just rule (see [Psalm 67:4](https://ref.ly/Ps67:4)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 67:3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 67:4 (id: 24756)

God delivers the ultimate justice that people seek ([Psalms 96:10](https://ref.ly/Ps96:10); [98:9](https://ref.ly/Ps98:9)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 67:4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Fairness (ID: `keyterm:Fairness`)

## Psalms 67:6–7 (id: 24757)

* Large harvests show God’s blessing ([Genesis 26:12](https://ref.ly/Gen26:12); [Isaiah 30:23](https://ref.ly/Isa30:23)).
* *all the ends of the earth*: See [Psalms 2:8](https://ref.ly/Ps2:8); [96:1–3](https://ref.ly/Ps96:1-Ps96:3).
* People from every nation will join Israel in acknowledging the God of Israel.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 67:6-7

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`)

## Psalms 68:1–3 (id: 24759)

These verses form a prayer for God to rise and scatter his enemies, like when Israel entered the promised land (compare [Numbers 10:35–36](https://ref.ly/Num10:35-Num10:36); [Joshua 6–12](https://ref.ly/Josh6:1-Josh12:24)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 68:1-3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 68:1–35 (id: 24758)

This prayer for God's victorious rule describes God's journey from Sinai to Zion ([Psalm 68:7](https://ref.ly/Ps68:7), [16–18](https://ref.ly/Ps68:16-Ps68:18), [24](https://ref.ly/Ps68:24)). He saved Israel from Egypt, led them through the wilderness, brought them to the promised land, and established his kingdom. He is a caring and victorious God, whose nature does not change. Therefore, the faithful hope and rejoice in the promise of God's universal rule.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 68:1-35

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Mount Sinai (ID: `place:SinaiMount`); Bashan (ID: `place:Bashan`); Out of Free Will (ID: `keyterm:OutOfFreeWill`); Money (ID: `realia:Money`); Community (ID: `keyterm:Community`); Fear (ID: `keyterm:Fear`); Zebulun (ID: `person:Zebulun`); Judah (ID: `person:Judah.4`); Benjamin (ID: `person:Benjamin`); Egypt (ID: `place:Egypt`); Tribe of Naphtali (ID: `group:Naphtali`); Jerusalem (ID: `place:Jerusalem`); Tribe of Benjamin (ID: `group:Benjamin`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); Tribe of Judah (ID: `group:Judah`); Cush (ID: `place:Cush.2`); Mount Ebal (ID: `place:EbalMount`); Tribe of Zebulun (ID: `group:Zebulun`); To Cause to Possess (ID: `keyterm:Possess`); Naphtali (ID: `person:Naphtali`); Molten Image (ID: `realia:MoltenImage`); Chariot (ID: `realia:Chariot`); Dog (ID: `fauna:Dog`); Cattle (ID: `fauna:Bull`); Reed (ID: `flora:Reed`); Dove (ID: `fauna:Dove`)

## Psalms 68:2 (id: 24774)

Both *smoke* and *wax* represent a temporary existence ([Psalms 22:12–18](https://ref.ly/Ps22:12-Ps22:18); [37:20](https://ref.ly/Ps37:20); [102:3](https://ref.ly/Ps102:3)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 68:2

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 68:3 (id: 24779)

The fate of the godly is different from that of the wicked (see [Psalm 1:6](https://ref.ly/Ps1:6)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 68:3

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 68:4 (id: 24781)

*Him who rides on the clouds*: This is an ancient description of Baal, a Canaanite god. The psalmist uses it to describe the Lord as the true God "who rides upon the highest heavens" (see [Psalms 68:33](https://ref.ly/Ps68:33); [104:3](https://ref.ly/Ps104:3)). God has power over the clouds and the rain.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 68:4

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 68:4–6 (id: 24780)

The psalmist praises God’s powerful and compassionate reign.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 68:4-6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 68:5 (id: 24782)

* God, the Father of all marginalized and needy people, shows them compassion and protects their rights (see [Psalms 10:14](https://ref.ly/Ps10:14), [18](https://ref.ly/Ps10:18); [69:33](https://ref.ly/Ps69:33); [82:3](https://ref.ly/Ps82:3)).
* Like his dwelling*,* the Lord is holy. The supreme example of the holy God coming down to rescue humanity is Jesus Christ.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 68:5

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 68:6 (id: 24783)

* God freed Israel from slavery in Egypt ([Exodus 20:2](https://ref.ly/Exod20:2); see [Psalm 69:33](https://ref.ly/Ps69:33)), and he continues to free prisoners today.
* The *sun\-scorched land* represents being far from God and separated from blessing.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 68:6

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 68:8–10 (id: 24784)

* The *earth shook* as it does during an earthquake (see [Exodus 19:18](https://ref.ly/Exod19:18)).
* *the heavens poured down rain*: Wells in the desert and rain showers in Canaan provided plenty of water.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 68:8-10

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Out of Free Will (ID: `keyterm:OutOfFreeWill`); Community (ID: `keyterm:Community`); Israel (ID: `person:Israel`); Mount Sinai (ID: `place:SinaiMount`); To Cause to Possess (ID: `keyterm:Possess`)

## Psalms 68:11 (id: 24760)

*a great company of women proclaim it*: A large group of women singers celebrates the victory in song (see [Psalm 68:25](https://ref.ly/Ps68:25); [Exodus 15:20](https://ref.ly/Exod15:20)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 68:11

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 68:13 (id: 24761)

The people who cared for flocks and moved from place to place were surprised to receive silver and gold ornaments from the battle.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 68:13

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Money (ID: `realia:Money`); Dove (ID: `fauna:Dove`)

## Psalms 68:14 (id: 24762)

* The Almighty (Hebrew *Shaddai*) is an ancient name for God (see [Psalm 91:1](https://ref.ly/Ps91:1); [Genesis 17:1](https://ref.ly/Gen17:1)).
* The exact location of Mount Zalmon is unknown; it might have been a mountain in Bashan (see [Psalm 68:15](https://ref.ly/Ps68:15)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 68:14

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Mount Ebal (ID: `place:EbalMount`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 68:15 (id: 24763)

Bashan is a plateau northeast of Jerusalem and east of the Sea of Galilee. It was famous for its forests and pastureland (see [Psalm 22:12](https://ref.ly/Ps22:12); [Isaiah 2:13](https://ref.ly/Isa2:13)). The mountains might have included Mount Hermon to the north of the plateau or Mount Zalmon, possibly Jebel ed\-Druze to the east.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 68:15

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Bashan (ID: `place:Bashan`)

## Psalms 68:16 (id: 24764)

*Why do you gaze in envy*: Bashan is more impressive than the mountains around Jerusalem, but it was not the Lord's home. Only Mount Zion had the honor of being God's dwelling place.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 68:16

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 68:17 (id: 24765)

Chariots were the top weapon of the military in the psalmist’s time. They represent the Lord’s power to give victory ([Psalms 20:7](https://ref.ly/Ps20:7); [44:3](https://ref.ly/Ps44:3)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 68:17

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Mount Sinai (ID: `place:SinaiMount`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`); Chariot (ID: `realia:Chariot`)

## Psalms 68:18 (id: 24766)

* *You have ascended on high*: The Most High victoriously established his reign in Zion.
* *captives … gifts*: This image shows a victorious ruler leading a victory march after battle. Paul applied this image to Jesus Christ ([Ephesians 4:8–13](https://ref.ly/Eph4:8-Eph4:13)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 68:18

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

## Psalms 68:19 (id: 24768)

Like a caring father or a shepherd, God carries his people in his arms (see [Psalm 55:22](https://ref.ly/Ps55:22); [68:5–6](https://ref.ly/Ps68:5-Ps68:6)).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 68:19

* **Associated ACAI Concepts:** Bless (ID: `keyterm:Bless`); Salvation (ID: `keyterm:Salvation`); LORD (ID: `deity:Lord`)

God cares for his people like a shepherd, and the people praise him.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 68:19-20

## Psalms 69:1–72.20 (id: 24787)

These last four psalms of Book Two contrast with the earlier psalms that describe God’s mighty acts in creation and redemption, ending with God’s march to Zion ([Psalms 65–68](https://ref.ly/Ps65:1-Ps68:35)). [Psalms 69–71](https://ref.ly/Ps69:1-Ps71:24) describe the king's suffering, and [Psalm 72](https://ref.ly/Ps72:1-Ps72:20) responds with a prayer for God to bless the king.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 69:1-72:20

The psalmist is devoted to God and what is holy, yet people repay him with insults. Later, this verse was applied to the anger of Jesus at the temple’s money changers ([John 2:17](https://ref.ly/John2:17)) and his undeserved suffering ([Romans 15:3](https://ref.ly/Rom15:3)).

*vinegar to quench my thirst*: This description applies to the suffering of Jesus ([Matthew 27:34](https://ref.ly/Matt27:34), [48](https://ref.ly/Matt27:48); [Luke 23:36](https://ref.ly/Luke23:36); [John 19:28–29](https://ref.ly/John19:28-John19:29)).
